# Project Status

> **Last updated:** 2026-05-14 (Thursday — session 1, mid-morning. **Calendar swipe animation shipped** — a UX polish on the existing horizontal-swipe gesture on the Planner's day/week/month surfaces. Previously: swipe past threshold → `nextDay()`/`nextWeek()`/`nextMonth()` fired and Vue flashed in the new content with zero motion. Now: an iOS-Calendar-style two-phase slide. New composable `src/composables/useCalendarSlide.ts` layered on top of the existing `useHorizontalSwipe` (which keeps owning gesture detection — its 13-test contract untouched). Behaviour: (a) live drag — element follows the finger via `translate3d(dx, 0, 0)`, motion past the 60px commit threshold is rubber-banded with coefficient 0.45 so it resists infinite pulling; (b) commit (released past threshold) — Web Animations API runs phase A (current `dx` → `−sign×width`, 220ms, easing `cubic-bezier(0.32, 0.72, 0, 1)` — Apple's system page-transition curve), then calls `onNext`/`onPrev` (Vue re-renders), instantly snaps to `+sign×width`, then phase B (off-screen edge → 0, 320ms); content swap happens while the element is off-screen so the user perceives one continuous slide; (c) cancel (released below threshold or pointercancel) — single 220ms ease back to 0; (d) `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` short-circuits the whole thing to instant content swap, no drag preview — matches the pre-animation behaviour exactly; (e) re-entrancy — `enabled` ref flips false during the commit animation so a second swipe can't fire mid-flight (wired through `useHorizontalSwipe`'s existing `enabled` parameter via a `computed`). Two implementation details: (1) `useHorizontalSwipe`'s terminal `onProgress(0)` (fires at end of every gesture, committed or cancelled) is disambiguated by a synchronous `committing` flag set inside `onSwipeLeft`/`onSwipeRight` (which fire before `onProgress(0)` in `onPointerUp`); when true, the composable's `onProgress` handler skips the spring-back. (2) Phase B waits one `requestAnimationFrame` after the synchronous swap so Vue's just-triggered re-render lands before the inbound animation kicks off — otherwise the new content can flash with the old layout for one frame. Wired into all three planner views — `DailyCalendarView.vue` / `WeeklyCalendarView.vue` / `CalendarGrid.vue` — each a 4-line swap (import + call + a `will-change: transform` hint added alongside the existing `touch-action: pan-y` for GPU compositing). 10 new unit tests (`src/composables/__tests__/useCalendarSlide.test.ts`) covering direction, drag preview, rubber-band damping, commit animation sequence, re-entrancy lockout, and the reduced-motion path. **Out of scope (documented in the plan)**: live peek of destination content during drag (the iOS Calendar "carousel of three" pattern — would require lifting `referenceDate` out of each view); animating prev/next button taps (greg's request was specifically about swipe); vertical-axis swipe between views. `npm run validate` clean (0 errors, 2213 unit tests pass, build 15.85s). Plan: `docs/plans/2026-05-14-calendar-swipe-animation.md`. **Not yet deployed — awaiting greg's verification on real hardware** (the slide-out/slide-in pacing is hard to assess from a desktop browser; intentional swipe-on-iPhone preview before shipping to prod). No commits yet either; staged as working-tree changes.)

`buildOwnerDoc()` factored out of `signUp` and shared with `rehydrateOwnerDoc` so the post-redirect resume path also restores `setOnboardingCompleted(false)` (the post-pod money/savings wizard now correctly appears after a resume — was being silently skipped); `🎉 Family pod created!` Slack ping moved from `CreatePodView.handleStep2Next` into `syncStore.createNewFile()` (fires for every storage type incl. local — closed the reported gap — and on the resume path); `AuthUser.displayName` cached on the persisted session so the resume screen pre-fills the actual typed name (not `email.split('@')[0]`); Drive-failure modal → Try again / Use a local file instead (no "OK"-only dead end); `features.ts` warns loudly in prod builds if `errorReporter` is off while a cloud feature is on; full error-reporting closed across onboarding (`app.redirectAuthCompletion`, `app.postInitNoData`, `app.onboardingZombieState`, `createPod.connectDrive`/`selectLocalFile`/`createNewFile`, `resumeSetup.*`); ADR-026 + memory `reference_ios_oauth.md`; GitHub issue **#213**. **(B) Chunk-load recovery saga** (`af1820f`, `2ec88eb`, `4b715b5`, `3bcbec8`, `e091b47` (reverted), `f9db1c2`, `d86e797`) — broadened `isChunkLoadError` regex to match iOS Safari's "from null or undefined value" wording (previous regex only matched V8/Chrome's "as it is null"); `hardReload()` now also `reg.unregister()`s the SW (closes the iOS "stale precache via stale SW" loop where evicting caches alone doesn't break the intercept on iOS WebKit); `handleReload` clears the once-guard so a user click can re-attempt recovery; `LoginPage.onMounted` waits for `authStore.isInitialized` AND a setup-level `watchEffect` reactively detects `?resume=setup` — closed two distinct races where LoginPage decided activeView before App.vue's async auth init / before App.vue's mid-mount `router.replace('/welcome?resume=setup')` landed (user was getting dropped on WelcomeGate instead of ResumePodSetup); deploy.yml drops `--delete` (so old chunks survive the CloudFront cache-stale window — was the actual root cause of the looping, not a client bug: CDN serving stale `index.html` referencing chunks just deleted by `--delete`) + re-uploads `index.html` with `Cache-Control: no-cache, max-age=0, must-revalidate` so CDN always revalidates instead of holding the stale shell for 24h (default\*ttl=86400 per `infrastructure/modules/app-subdomain/main.tf`); workbox `cleanupOutdatedCaches:true`; **`CHUNK_RELOAD_FLAG` is now a counter (max 3 silent hardReloads across all 3 callsites — App.vue catch / router.onError / vite:preloadError)**, cleared only after a successful post-init health check (NOT on every `router.afterEach` — that fired before the error could throw, defeating the bound); attempt 4 → tappable overlay + `app.chunkRecoveryFailed` Slack alert with attempt count + route. **Tried and reverted** mid-saga: workbox `skipWaiting + clientsClaim` (`e091b47`) — fights `registerType:'prompt'` per vite-plugin-pwa docs (new SW claims current page mid-precache-install → asset fetches resolve to null states), made greg's recovery worse, reverted in `f9db1c2`. **Verified live end-to-end on greg's iPhone 14 Safari (iOS 18.7)**: Drive consent → return → bounded recovery (1-2 silent iterations) → `<ResumePodSetup>` with the actual name pre-filled → password → `/nook` → onboarding wizard. `#beanies-errors` now lights up correctly: `🫘 New family pod started!` + `🎉 Family pod created!` for every successful pod creation (both storage types); `app.onboardingZombieState` per resume round-trip (expected — the safety net working); zero `app.chunkRecoveryFailed` in steady state. **Plan + prompt archived**: `docs/plans/2026-05-13-onboarding-iphone-drive-hardening.md`, `docs/prompts/2026-05/2026-05-13-onboarding-iphone-drive-hardening.md`. **Out of scope / follow-up** (in Pending below): "Move to Google Drive" migration of an \_existing* pod on iOS (hidden on iOS/PWAs until the same redirect-auth dance is wired with the family key); Someday items are excluded from the Nook To-Do widget, the critical-activities briefing (`useCriticalItems`), the planner sidebar (`TodoPreview`), the Nook Schedule cards (`ScheduleCards`), and the calendar grid; they stay in the To-Do page's Someday section + global search. 6 new `uiStrings` keys (`npm run translate` synced zh); new `src/stores/__tests__/todoStore.test.ts` (4 tests) + `src/components/todo/__tests__/TodoItemRow.test.ts` (2); `npm run validate` clean (type-check / lint / format / tests / build). Help: new "Someday · Maybe to-dos" section in the **Family To-Do Lists** article. Plan: `docs/plans/2026-05-12-someday-maybe-todos.md`. — Earlier this day: **Storage Provider Migration shipped & deployed (Vue prod `25707007190` + Astro prod `25706883712`).** Closes a real product gap: families could pick a storage type (local file vs Google Drive) at onboarding but had no in-app way to change it later — the only workaround was download `.beanpod` → sign out → re-onboard. **(1) New `syncStore.migrateStorage(target)`** (`src/stores/syncStore.ts`) — owner-only, returns a discriminated `MigrateStorageResult` (`success` / `cancelled` / `failed` / `recovery-needed`); flat `try/catch/finally`; flushes the source one last time (non-forced `syncNow`, so a cross-device conflict surfaces rather than getting clobbered) → builds the destination provider via `buildProviderForTarget` (null on user-cancel of a picker/chooser) → installs it → on install failure re-installs the previous provider via `restorePreviousProvider`; if the rollback itself fails, returns `recovery-needed` (the toast tells the user to sign out and back in). Encrypted bytes are unchanged — same family key, same envelope, no password prompt; the source file is left intact as a backup (we never delete it). Telemetry funnel: `reportError({surface:'storage-migration-ok', severity:'warning'})` on success, `{surface:'storage-migration-failed', severity:'error', context:{from,to,step}}` on failure (`step` ∈ `pre-swap`/`install`/`rollback`). **(2) DRY refactor underneath it** — extracted the duplicated "make this provider active" sequence out of `configureSyncFile` + `configureSyncFileGoogleDrive` into a shared private `installProvider(provider, type)` (persist → setProvider → syncNow → saveSettings → registerCurrentFamily → setupAutoSync/setupTokenExpiryHandler; throws on the first failing step; the `isReloading` guard around syncNow+saveSettings preserved). Side effect: a previously-silent `syncNow`-returns-false in `configureSyncFile` now throws (no silent failures). Extracted `isUserCancellation(e)` into `googleAuth.ts` (matches `AbortError` by name + the GIS/popup-closed message shapes); `handleSwitchGoogleAccount` rewired onto it, dropping its inline regex. **(3) `GoogleDriveProvider.createNew(name, { forceConsent? })`** — `forceConsent` defaults `true` (zero change for onboarding); the migration passes `false` so a cached token is reused — `prompt=consent` forces the redirect-auth path on a standalone PWA even with a cached token, which would loop on the post-redirect retry of a local→Drive move; with `forceConsent:false` the retry completes without re-redirecting. The earlier-drafted standalone pre-flight `requestAccessToken()` was dropped during implementation in favour of this (one fewer cancellation site). **(4) UI** — owner-gated "Move to Google Drive" / "Move to a local file" action row in the Family Data modal (`SettingsPage.vue`), `useConfirm` info-variant dialog (Heritage Orange, routine not destructive), result-driven toasts passed `{silent:true}` so they don't double-ping `#beanies-errors` (the store already reports the failure with full context). **(5) i18n** — 18 new `uiStrings` keys under `settings.familyData.migrate.*` + `action.move` (en + beanie each); `npm run translate` synced Chinese. **(6) Help Center** (per the updated `/beanies-plan` skill — Help coverage now folded into plans) — new how-to article `moving-your-pod-storage` in `src/content/help/getting-started.ts` (owner-only; old file stays as a backup; other devices must re-load from the new location; no password prompt; what to do if it tells you to sign out/in); cross-reference + `updatedDate` bump on `connecting-google-drive`; SKILL.md inventory 4→6 (also picked up the previously-missing `install-as-app`). **(7) `/beanies-plan` skill update** (`b6bab02`-style follow-up to the skill itself) — defaults to direct implementation (no GitHub issue unless explicitly asked — one-dev project); adds a "Help Center coverage" assessment + a `## Help Center Coverage` section to the plan template. **Tests**: new `src/stores/__tests__/syncStore.migrate.test.ts` (10 tests — both happy paths, both cancellations, pre-swap flush failure, install-failure-with-rollback, rollback-failure→recovery-needed, guard rails + re-entrancy, plus `configureSyncFileGoogleDrive` exercising `installProvider`); `isUserCancellation` cases added to `googleAuth.test.ts`. 2136 unit tests passing (+13); type-check + lint + build + translate clean. Plan saved to `docs/plans/2026-05-12-storage-provider-migration.md`. **Deploy**: single commit `3a457e4`; Main CI 3m19s ✓, Security ✓, Vue Deploy ~1m40s ✓, Astro Deploy ~56s ✓. **Note on connectivity**: a couple of transient API hiccups again — once `/deploy-prod-auto` appeared to hang with no output (stalled before the workflow-trigger step; nothing in flight, nothing broke — re-running recovered it). **Pending into next session**: manual browser smoke of the move flows (Chromium + Safari, plus the Safari-PWA local→Drive redirect case) — not doable from CLI, flagged below; telemetry watch for `storage-migration-*` in `#beanies-errors`; rest of pending block validated below — removed the long-carried "Activity photos" follow-up bullet (shipped 2026-05-11; `registerPhotoCollection('activities')` confirmed in `App.vue:79`).)

> **Earlier 2026-05-12 (Tuesday — for archival reference):** **Public holidays on the Family Planner — shipped & deployed 2026-05-12** (Vue prod `25731544444`; greg smoke-tested in dev — country picker in **Settings → Country & Holidays**, holidays on the month/week/day calendar views + the planner sidebar, the show/hide toggle, "Not set", offline degrade, vacation+holiday days — all OK). Tell beanies.family where the family lives (onboarding step 1 or Settings) and the planner marks that country's national `public` holidays — display-only, never added to the calendar, never editable. **What landed:** `CountryCode` type + `Settings.country?` / `Settings.showPublicHolidays?` (+ `GlobalSettings.country?`; > **Earlier 2026-05-10 (Saturday wrap-up — for archival reference):** closing out an all-day push that crossed midnight. Three Vue prod deploys + one Astro prod deploy across three feature/fix tracks. **(1) Travel-plan airport dropdown expansion (`041ab46`)** — replaced ~200 hand-curated airports with **~4,200 commercial airports globally** generated from [OurAirports](https://ourairports.com/data/) public-domain data filtered to `scheduled_service=yes` + valid IATA code (covers HGH, CKG, GMP, and every other airport with regular commercial flights; general-aviation strips like TOA Torrance excluded — combobox's "Other" entry handles that case). New `scripts/updateAirports.mjs` regen script (fetches CSV → filters → cleans names → strips parenthetical city suburbs → renders single-quoted TS → runs Prettier; idempotent — no write on no-change) + monthly GitHub Action `airport-sync.yml` runs 1st of each month at 04:00 UTC and opens a PR if upstream data changed. `AirportInfo` gains optional `country` (ISO alpha-2) for future disambiguation UI. Vue prod deploy `25599317866` (1m 34s). **(2) Large reading mode end-to-end (six commits `b3e9ce8` → `f6ef505`, plus `601e40c` prettier fix)** — full feature shipped after a multi-pass plan review (DRY/error-handling → sustainability/maintainability → fresh-eyes pass) and a live HTML mockup at `public/mockup-large-text.html` served on the dev server for visual review at 4 reference widths (375/393/768/1440) before any production code touched. Architecture: single CSS rule `html[data-text-size='large'] { font-size: calc(1rem * var(--text-scale-large)) }` in `style.css:33` reading from a single source of truth (`--text-scale-large: 1.1875` token in `packages/brand/theme.css:60`). Because every Tailwind v4 utility and custom rem value in the codebase is rem-based, the entire UI rescales (16 px → 19 px root, 1.1875×) from that one rule — no per-component variant. **Phase 2 cleanup sweep** (four sub-commits, each independently reviewable, all preserve Normal-mode appearance): 2a converted **186 `text-[Xpx]` arbitrary classes across 64 components** to `text-[Xrem]` (px/16 = rem, mathematically exact for every value present); 2b converted **6 body-content `font-size: Xpx` declarations** to rem and marked **13 decorative ones** (hero confetti, fixed-size icon containers, arrow glyphs in buttons, brand-mark elements) with explicit `/* stylelint-disable-next-line declaration-property-value-disallowed-list -- decorative ... */` comments naming the reason (the lint rule itself lands in Phase 3); 2c reframed `MobileBottomNav.vue:169` `min-h-[56px]` → `min-h-14` (Tailwind v4 spacing token, identical at default root, scales to ~67 px in Large) and converted calendar `ROW_HEIGHT` constant from `60` (px) to `3.75` (rem units); the three calendar consumers (`DailyCalendarView`/`WeeklyCalendarView`/`DayTimeline`) now interpolate `${expr}rem` for `top`/`height` styles, and the `scrollTop` math at the auto-scroll-to-now call sites multiplies by the live root font-size (read once via `getComputedStyle`) since `scrollTop` requires pixels; 2d added **`src/composables/useChartScale.ts`** (single subscription, leak-proof — uses `MutationObserver` on the `<html data-text-size>` attribute, registers/unregisters via Vue's `onMounted`/`onBeforeUnmount` so forgetting to unregister is impossible) wired into `NetWorthHeroCard.vue` + `ReportsPage.vue` (Line + Bar). Each chart adds **one line** in setup: `useChartScale(chartRef)`. Couples nothing: chart components don't import the store, the store doesn't know about charts. **Phase 1 plumbing** (`9af3ea5`) introduced `persistDualSetting<K>()` helper in `settingsStore.ts` that collapses the previously-duplicated 14-line dual-write try/catch from `setTheme` + `setLanguage` into one well-tested code path — toast on failure (with retry action via `showToast('error', t('settings.persistFailed'), fieldLabel, { actionLabel: t('common.retry'), actionFn: () => persistDualSetting(key, value), error, surface: 'settings-persist', context: { field } })`), `error.value` populated, console.error with field+attempted value+stack, exception re-thrown so `BaseSelect` can revert visual state. `setTheme`/`setLanguage`/`setTextSize` are now one-line wrappers. New `textSize` field on `Settings` and `GlobalSettings` (additive, no migration; `?? 'normal'` fallback). New watcher with **flicker guard** (no-op on first immediate fire if the bootstrap script already applied the correct attribute). **Anti-FOUC bootstrap script** in `index.html` reads persisted `theme` + `textSize` from localStorage synchronously before any CSS loads — pays down a pre-existing dark-mode FOUC bug as a bonus. **Storage keys** consolidated in new `src/constants/storageKeys.ts` (`STORAGE_KEYS.TEXT_SIZE`, `STORAGE_KEYS.THEME`); inline bootstrap duplicates the literals with a cross-reference comment (inline scripts can't import). **Phase 3 tooling/docs** (`f6ef505`): Stylelint `declaration-property-value-disallowed-list` rule forbids `font-size: Xpx` (`.stylelintrc.json:21-29`); ESLint `vue/no-restricted-class` rule forbids `text-[Xpx]` arbitrary classes in templates (`eslint.config.js:46-50`); both rule messages link to SKILL.md § Text-size accessibility mode and document the decorative opt-out comment pattern. SKILL.md gained a "Text-size accessibility mode" subsection in Typography (line 87) codifying the rem rule + `--text-scale-large` token. **CIG slide added** — `docs/brand/beanies-cig-v2.html` got a new "Accessibility & Modes" slide (id="s7") between Typography and Tagline documenting Dark + Beanie + Large together; trailing slides renumbered 7→8…16→17. CLAUDE.md got a one-liner under Code Conventions. **Beanie tip-of-the-day** for Large mode added alongside the Dark-mode tip in `src/content/tips.ts` (id `tip-large-text`, /settings deep-link). All 6 new translation keys (`settings.textSize`, `.normal`, `.large`, `.textSizeHint`, `settings.persistFailed`, `common.retry`) parsed by `npm run translate` and translated to Chinese cleanly. Astro prod deploy (Astro picked up the `--text-scale-large` brand token), Vue prod deploy `25602886780` (~2 min). One mid-flight CI fix `601e40c` (prettier flagged the bootstrap script's `isDark = ...` ternary at 102 chars — auto-formatted to two-line wrap; pure cosmetic, no behaviour change). **(3) E2E webkit harden — sixth recurrence of `AccountsPage.selectAccountType` flake (`5beac12`)** — three webkit-only hard-fails (cross-entity.spec.ts:23/416 + planner.spec.ts:261) on push commit `601e40c` (a 2-line wrap-only style fix); chromium 21/21 green; `gh run rerun --failed` passed cleanly on the second attempt — confirming pure flake, not a regression. Per the 2026-05-05 E2E_HEALTH guidance for the 30s-timeout recurrence, the helper now polls for the **"Select a type" hint text** (rendered only inside the expanded category block per `AccountCategoryPicker.vue:135`) before waiting for the subtype button. The hint becoming visible means the v-show display:none flip + a11y-tree recompute have completed, so the subsequent subtype `waitFor` returns immediately on the happy path. Failure mode shifts from "ambiguous subtype timeout at 30s" to "expansion-stall surfaced in <5s" — real bugs fail fast, flakes still get the full retry budget. E2E_HEALTH log updated with the 6th-recurrence entry. **(4) Production OAuth-fetch hang fix (`2c86108`)** — `/error-review` triage of three `cold-start-reconnect-escalation` Slack alerts from greg's iPhone PWA in 4 hours, plus user-reported frozen "counting beans..." spinner after manually reconnecting (force-killing the PWA was the only escape, after which the cycle repeated). Triaged GENUINE: both fetches in `src/services/google/oauthProxy.ts` (`exchangeCodeForTokens` for popup/redirect token exchange, `refreshAccessToken` for silent refresh) called `fetch()` with **no network timeout**. On iOS Safari over flaky cellular / Wi-Fi handover, `fetch` can hang for minutes or never resolve — and because `attemptSilentRefresh` deduplicates concurrent callers via `pendingSilentRefresh`, a single hung fetch pinned every subsequent caller to the same dead promise. The 4s cold-start defer still fired the reconnect banner, but clicking Reconnect ran `completeRedirectAuth()` → another untimedout `exchangeCodeForTokens` fetch → app init wedged at `isInitializing=true` so the full-screen spinner never dismissed. The 2026-05-08 silent-refresh hardening (`8152872`) lowered the failure-counter threshold 3 → 2 and made escalation faster — that didn't cause the hang, it just exposed it earlier, matching greg's observation that reconnect prompts had been "happening much more than before." Fix: new `fetchWithTimeout(url, init, timeoutMs = 15_000)` helper using `AbortController`. Both fetches go through it. Timeout error message includes the substring "silent refresh failed" so `isAuthTransientSyncError` (`syncStore.ts:290-292`) routes the failure as auth-transient → reconnect-banner path keeps working AND wake-event recovery hooks fire. Defense-in-depth: `App.vue:512` wraps the `completeRedirectAuth()` call in a 20s `Promise.race` so init can never wedge even if a future code path adds another awaited fetch. Three new unit tests pin the timeout behaviour. Vue prod deploy `25603971921` (~1m 45s: gate 3s + build 1m18s + S3+CloudFront 22s). New noise-list entry `oauth-proxy-fetch-hang` added to `reference_known_noise.md` so future triages cross-reference. **Test status throughout**: 2061 → 2064 unit tests passing (+3 from oauthProxy timeout tests); type-check + lint + build clean across all 10 commits. **Other-terminal commits**: none today. **Pending into next session**: telemetry watch on the OAuth-fetch fix (next `cold-start-reconnect-escalation` alert, if any, will end with "OAuth proxy fetch timed out after 15000ms" — clean signal that proxy/network is bottlenecked, not our code; alerts with the old shape would mean the timeout didn't catch); Mother's Day weekend amplification active TODAY (Day 10 of 14 of aloe vera launch — Quora answer Sun 8-10 PM SGT + 2nd Substack Note); rest of pending block validated and trimmed below.)

> **Earlier 2026-05-08 (Friday — for archival reference):** five-commit session across two engineering tracks plus active launch-comms work. Two Vue prod deploys (`8152872` silent-refresh regression closure + `95e3ae0` CSS structural cleanup), one Astro prod deploy (`eabb7ac` activity-finance-linking blog post + featured-card height fix + post-publish content tweaks). **(1) Silent-refresh regression fix (`8152872`)** — greg opened the laptop in the morning and reproduced exactly the symptom he reported the night before: stale cache, no reconnect toast, no save-failure banner, only the yellow "silent refresh failed" message buried in Settings → Family Data. Root-cause was a five-way gap in the post-`e5fee92`/`b240f6e` architecture: (a) the consecutive-failure counter at `googleAuth.ts:74` was module-level state — every page reload reset it to 0, so reload-loops never accumulated to threshold; (b) threshold of 3 was 27 OAuth proxy fetches across 3 separate `attemptSilentRefresh` calls before escalation; (c) `backgroundSyncFromFile` already classified the failure as `auth-transient` (`syncStore.ts:1320-1335`) but explicitly suppressed UI, deferring to the auth-layer counter that would never cross; (d) wake listener only fired on `visibilitychange`, missing `focus`/`pageshow` (BFCache restore on mobile)/`online`; (e) offline queue retried only on `online` event, so an auth failure with healthy network sat forever. Plus a pre-existing silent failure: `getGoogleRefreshToken` rejection in `performSilentRefresh` could throw past the wake listener's `.catch(() => {})`. Fix: counter persisted to sessionStorage so reload-loops accumulate; threshold lowered to 2 with `>=` (defensive against overshoot races); array-driven `WAKE_EVENTS` covering visibilitychange/focus/pageshow/online; new ~4s deferred banner-fire from `backgroundSyncFromFile`'s auth-transient branch via a `createDeferredAction(ms)` factory that ALSO replaced the existing inline save-failure-banner timer plumbing (one primitive, two consumers); offline queue gains a `tryFlush(reason)` helper plus `onTokenAcquired` and `visibilitychange→visible` recovery hooks alongside the existing `online`. IDB-read try/catch closes the silent failure. Five new `reportError` surfaces for telemetry: `silent-refresh-counter-storage`, `silent-refresh-idb-read`, `cold-start-reconnect-escalation`, `syncStore-deferred-action`, `syncStore-reconnect-clear`, `offline-queue-flush`. 13 new unit tests, 2 existing tests updated for threshold change. **Test status pre-push**: 2061 unit tests pass / type-check clean / translate up-to-date. **Production telemetry confirmed working**: post-deploy at 11:19 SG, two `cold-start-reconnect-escalation` alerts fired in `#beanies-errors` exactly as designed (greg's tab on the new build, deferred ~4s window correctly surfaced the banner). Triaged via `/error-review`: verdict GENUINE — system worked as designed. **(2) Activity-finance-linking blog post (`0a748fe`/`dc5145c`/`eabb7ac`)** — Notion content database item #9 ("It must be nice to have beanies by your side") shipped to the beanstalk as `content/blog/2026-05-08-activity-finance-linking.md` with the activity-edit modal screenshot at `web/public/blog/activity-finance-linking.png`. Featured-card height fix on `/blog`: the card's `.issue-featured .issue-media` had `aspect-ratio: auto` + `min-height: 340px` which let a portrait cover image (873×1578) drive the grid row to ~900px, eating the page. Restored `aspect-ratio: 16/10` with a `max-height: 480px` ceiling; switched featured-card image to `object-fit: contain` so portrait screenshots render in full inside the kraft polaroid frame instead of cover-cropping out the orange highlights. Aloe-vera flipped to `featured: false` so the new post anchors the homepage gradient card alone. **(3) CSS structural cleanup (`95e3ae0`)** — yesterday's deferred session. Two-pass plan rigor (DRY/no-silent-failures + sustainability) before execution. Stage A: wrapped the four unlayered groups in `style.css` (`html, body`, `body { ... font-family ... }`, `#app`, the `.beanies-input` cluster including `select.beanies-input` + date/time + the `@supports ::picker(select)` block) in `@layer base` so utility classes can override per element — closes the cascade-layer trap class that bit yesterday's Caveat fix. `@supports` nested inside `@layer base` not the other way around for one less nesting level. Body's hardcoded `font-family: Inter, system-ui, ...` chain now reads from `var(--font-inter)` token. Legacy `:root` color tokens (`--color-primary` / `--color-primary-dark` / `--heritage-orange` / `--color-secondary`) now derive from the `--color-*-{500,600,700}` scale in `packages/brand/theme.css` (single source of truth; resolved hexes identical, no caller rewrites). `.attention-pulse` joined the reduced-motion kill-list. Stage B: rigor pass surfaced that NookSectionCard was the LONE outlier — every other card-shaped component in the repo (14+ — BeanSpread, BeanSayingsTab, BeanAllergiesTab, BeanMedicationsTab, BeanFavoritesTab, BeanMilestonesTab, ScrapbookEntryCard, EveryoneSpread, BudgetPage ×3, RecipeDetailPage ×2, SummaryStatCard, StatTile) uses `class="rounded-[var(--sq)] bg-white p-5 shadow-[var(--card-shadow)] dark:bg-slate-800"`. NookSectionCard's hardcoded `rounded-[20px]` + inline shadow + `nook-card-dark` class (the source of the `.dark .nook-card-dark { ... !important }` global rule AND the cascading `!important`s in `NookVacationCard`) were aligned to the canonical pattern — slight rounded-corner increase from 20px to 24px (`var(--sq)`), consistent dark-mode shadow. ContentSkeleton.vue copied the outlier per its own comment and is realigned in lockstep. The dead `.dark .nook-card-dark { ... !important }` rule retired entirely. NookVacationCard's two `!important` retired (Vue scoped style `[data-v]` specificity boost is sufficient). Stage C: GoalsPage's `.progress-fill-green !important` rule replaced with Tailwind gradient utilities toggled in the `:class` binding (`from-green-500 to-emerald-500` vs `from-primary-500 to-terracotta-500`); onboarding BaseCombobox/BaseSelect overrides moved out of the global `onboarding-shared.css` into the owning components' (`OnboardingAccount.vue` for `ob-bank-combo`, `OnboardingRecurring.vue` for `ob-account-select`) `<style scoped>` blocks where the `[data-v]` specificity boost retires the four `!important` workarounds without touching the shared `BaseCombobox`/`BaseSelect` components. Net coupling decreases: onboarding-specific styles stop leaking into the global namespace. **`!important` count: 19 → 11** (8 smells retired); the remaining 11 are all justified per the audit (7 reduced-motion universal kill-switch + 4 transform-vs-hover overrides on tilted scrapbook cards). **Test status pre-push**: 2061 unit tests pass / type-check / lint / stylelint / translate all green. **Vue prod deploy `25551502088`**: 1m 36s total (verify 4s · build 1m 10s · S3+CloudFront 22s). **(4) Launch-comms work** (greg-driven, surfaced via direct asks): (a) Aloe Vera Submittable check this morning — Good Men Project ACCEPTED the piece, Audacity/Narratively/Longreads/The Sun still pending; reach validation via SimilarWeb showed GMP at ~118K monthly visits (NOT the 3M widely-cited stale figure), well below the four pending pubs' aggregate reach + pay; greg replied to GMP and Deb Harman/Wind Phone asking both to hold pending the higher-tier verdicts. > **Earlier 2026-05-07 (Thursday — for archival reference):** one focused engineering session: a feature-and-redesign deploy that had been queued in the working tree, plus a font/cascade-layer fix that surfaced during visual review, plus a CSS structural audit deferred for tomorrow's dedicated session. Two commits, one Vue prod deploy. **(1) Milestones feature + Family Scrapbook redesign** — `eb03538` is a 43-file multi-feature commit: new Milestones surface (Pinia store `milestonesStore.ts`, Automerge repository `milestoneRepository.ts`, type-discriminated `MilestoneFormModal.vue`, new bean tab `BeanMilestonesTab.vue`, family-wide timeline at `/family/timeline`, `useMilestoneLightbox` module-level singleton mirroring `useQuickAdd`'s pattern, 18 categories grouped Firsts/Achievements/Family/Celebrations); plus the Family Scrapbook redesign (`/pod/scrapbook`) from flat masonry feed to tabbed-spreads-with-page-flip — new components `ScrapbookSpine`, `ScrapbookEntryCard` (single-source-of-truth for entry-type → renderer dispatch via discriminated union), `EveryoneSpread` (cover with "Lately" hero block + per-bean clusters + family-moments footer), `BeanSpread` (per-bean magazine layout); page-level page-turn `<Transition>` with directional 3D rotateY pivot (left edge for forward turns, right for backward); CSS-only kraft paper / washi tape / taped+ripped paper utilities; pets are first-class members in the spine and per-bean clusters; photo-bearing milestones get polaroid-frame emphasis with grid-dense 2x2 spans. Two `docs/plans/2026-05-07-*.md` saved. Plus today's session-only fixes: three `sortedHumans` → `sortedMembers` swaps in `FamilyScrapbookPage.vue` (spine prop + `spreadIndex()` helper + activeguard watcher) so pets show up in the spine. **(2) Caveat font cascade-layer fix** — visual review revealed the "Our family scrapbook" `<h2>` rendering as Outfit instead of Caveat. Diagnosed in three layers: (a) Google Fonts URL only loaded Caveat at weights 500+700; unweighted `font-caveat` requested weight 400 → fell through to Outfit. Added `400` to the `Caveat:wght@` segment of `index.html`. (b) After URL fix the title still rendered Outfit because of a deeper bug: `src/style.css:421-425` had a global `h1, h2, h3 { font-family: Outfit, sans-serif }` rule **outside** any `@layer`. In CSS cascade-layer ordering, unlayered styles beat layered styles regardless of selector specificity — so the unlayered element rule won over Tailwind v4's `@layer utilities` `.font-caveat { ... }` even though class selectors normally beat element selectors. Wrapped the heading rule in `@layer base { ... }` so utility classes can override per element. (c) Defense-in-depth: added explicit `font-medium` on the title h2, family-name subtitle, page-header subtitle, and bean-polaroid caption — weight 500 was already loaded so works even on PWA-cached old assets. **(3) CSS structural audit (deferred)** — greg asked for a quick pass on whether the same cascade-layer trap lurks elsewhere; identified four findings: (a) THREE more unlayered element-selector groups in `style.css` ready to bite us next time someone applies a utility on those elements — `html, body { ... }` (lines 400-418, body font-family + bg + color literal), `input[type='date'], input[type='time'] { ... }` (479-502, font-family + accent-color + picker indicator styling), `select.beanies-input { ... }` (469); (b) parallel color-token systems — `packages/brand/theme.css` defines `--color-primary-500`/etc as Tailwind v4 scale; `src/style.css` defines `--color-primary` (no scale, legacy) — same hexes, components reference whichever was current when written, divergence risk if anyone updates one without the other; (c) `body` hardcodes `font-family: Inter, system-ui, ...` instead of `var(--font-inter)` (token defined in brand but not used here); (d) `!important` patterns: `NookVacationCard.vue:103,107` + `onboarding-shared.css:423,429,440,449` are fighting scoped styles in shared components (right fix: expose CSS variables on those components), `GoalsPage.vue:873` has unexplained `!important` on a progress gradient, `style.css:360 .dark .nook-card-dark { background: ... !important }` could become a `dark:bg-*` utility. Held all of this for tomorrow's dedicated session per greg's call. **(4) Deploy** — Vue prod deploy `25494835607` (1m 34s total: verify 4s · build 1m 6s · S3+CloudFront 24s). One pre-push hiccup: `eb03538`'s commit's lint-staged pre-commit hook ran prettier-plugin-tailwindcss on `EveryoneSpread.vue` and produced a malformed inline-style attribute (literal newlines mid-value on the `--washi-color` override) that CI's prettier then flagged as an error — root cause is a plugin quirk when wrapping long inline `style=""` attributes. Worked around in `96b7b93` by removing the redundant inline override (the value matched the CSS-default `.scrap-washi { --washi-color: ... }` from `style.css`, so the override was a no-op). Plugin bug itself unfixed; flagged for tomorrow's CSS session. **Test status**: 2040 unit tests passing across both pre-push runs (1985 → 2040 from milestones / scrapbook / family-timeline test additions in `eb03538`); type-check + lint + build clean on `96b7b93`. **Other-terminal commits**: none today. **Evening session (~21:00 SG, commit `b240f6e`)** — greg surfaced TWO production bugs. **(A) Sync silent-refresh escalation gap**: greg added a milestone on his iOS PWA, hard-reloaded desktop several times, didn't see the data — and noted the indeterminate `BackgroundSyncBar` never appeared on reload. Settings → Family Data showed a "data file not connected" card; one tap on his email via the reconnect popup brought everything back. Diagnosed: silent-refresh path was failing for transient reasons (proxy hiccup / refresh-token-not-loaded race / network jitter beyond the ~4.5s 3-attempt retry budget), classified as `auth-transient` in `backgroundSyncFromFile` (the toast is suppressed on that kind because the _design assumption_ was that `setupTokenExpiryHandler` would escalate). But the auth layer ONLY escalates to `permanentFailureCallbacks` (which fires `showGoogleReconnect = true`) on a definitive `invalid_grant` — see `googleAuth.ts:614-636`. Any other persistent failure mode silently exhausted retries and returned `null` with no UI signal. The "tap email once" reconnect succeeded because `useGoogleReconnect.reconnect` falls through silent-refresh → `attemptSilentAuthCode` (silent iframe, `prompt=none`, uses Google session cookies) → `performPopupAuth` (popup with `prompt=select_account`, also session-cookie based) — those paths are independent of the refresh-token+proxy chain that was broken. Fix: new module-level `consecutiveSilentRefreshFailures` counter in `googleAuth.ts` increments on each retry-exhausted `attemptSilentRefresh` call and fires `permanentFailureCallbacks` exactly when the counter crosses `SILENT_REFRESH_FAILURE_ESCALATION_THRESHOLD = 3` (uses `===` not `>=` so subsequent failures past threshold don't re-fire). Resets on any successful token acquisition via `notifyTokenAcquired` (covers silent + popup + redirect + silent auth-code paths via single point) and on `clearTokenState`. With file polling firing every 10s + ~4.5s retry budget per call, the banner now surfaces within ~30-45s of the system being stuck. Existing `onTokenAcquired` self-heal in syncStore (lines 1779-1799) auto-clears the surface when a later refresh succeeds, so a recovered transient blip doesn't leave a stale banner. Also factored the inline `forEach` from the `invalid_grant` branch into a `firePermanentFailureCallbacks()` helper. Three test changes in `googleAuth.test.ts`: renamed existing "fires onTokenPermanentlyExpired ONLY on invalid_grant" → "fires immediately on invalid_grant — but NOT on a single retry-exhausted transient failure" (assertion still holds — one retry-exhausted call is counter=1, below threshold); added "escalates to onTokenPermanentlyExpired after N consecutive retry-exhausted failures" (12 transient throws across 4 calls, fake timers + `vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(5000)` to skip 4.5s backoffs without triggering the ~55min `scheduleAutoRefresh` setTimeout); added "resets the consecutive-failure counter on a successful refresh" (2 fail → 1 success → 2 fail; counter goes 1, 2, 0, 1, 2 — no callback fires). Also added `vi.useRealTimers()` to afterEach as a fake-timer-leak safety net. Replaced `mockImplementation` with queued `mockImplementationOnce` calls because the former persists across `vi.resetModules` and was polluting `clearGoogleSessionState` / `onTokenAcquired` / `migratePendingRefreshToken` tests downstream. **(B) Scrapbook horizontal overflow**: greg reported the `/pod/scrapbook` PWA panned horizontally on mobile to reveal a strip of background — only this page, no other. Root cause: every entry card in `EveryoneSpread.vue` and `BeanSpread.vue` carries an inline `transform: rotate(±X deg)` for the tilt aesthetic. Rotated elements don't change their flow width but DO extend their visual bounding box past their grid cell; the body's `scrollWidth` includes that visual extent, so the browser allowed horizontal panning to reveal it. Other pages don't tilt content, which is why this was scrapbook-only. Fix: added `overflow-x: clip` (NOT `hidden`) to `.scrapbook-paper` in `src/style.css:574`. `clip` clips visually without creating a horizontal scroll context — preserves the sticky AppHeader and any future sticky descendants. **Test status**: 2042 / 2042 unit tests passing (+2 from auth escalation), type-check clean. **Vue prod deploy `25500883230`**: 1m 42s end-to-end (CI/Security verify 4s · build 1m 15s · S3+CloudFront 23s). **Astro deploy**: skipped (no marketing-site files changed). **Other-terminal commits**: none.)

> **Earlier 2026-05-06 update** (Wednesday — long day spread across engineering + ops + content/launch strategy. Six commits, six Vue prod deploys (three via `/deploy-prod-auto-skip-ci` for tight UX iterations, three full-CI), plus dependabot triage and substantial Notion launch-plan work. **(1) /create direct route** — `8136de8` adds `/create` to mirror `/join`'s pattern (5-line additive route + `create.title` translation key, auto-synced to Chinese via `npm run translate`). LoginPage already supported `initialView: 'create'`; `/welcome` selector remains default for ambiguous entries. **(2) MIME fix for .beanpod files** — `dc1f24d` is the actual root cause for Google Marketplace review issue #1 (Drive integration not appearing). Legacy `.beanpod` files were uploaded with `mimeType: 'application/json'` even though the V4 envelope is encrypted binary. Drive's content-sniff fell back to 'File Type: unknown' and the Marketplace SDK matcher refused to surface 'Open with...' for those files. Fix multi-pronged: `createFile` default + `updateFile` Content-Type both flip to `application/octet-stream`; new `patchFileMetadata` Drive API helper for metadata-only PATCH; new `ensureCorrectMimeType` once-per-session migration in `GoogleDriveProvider.read` that opportunistically PATCHes legacy files. Idempotent + non-critical (failures swallowed, retried next session). 6 new tests. Post-deploy verified in greg's Drive: console emitted `[GoogleDriveProvider] migrated .beanpod mimeType: application/json → application/octet-stream <fileId>`. Drive UI still shows 'unknown' file type (UI-side label heuristic, decoupled from underlying metadata which is now correct). **(3) Silent OAuth + cold-arrival /open UX** — `a679a99` addresses Google review issues #2 + #3. New internal `attemptSilentAuthCode` in `googleAuth.ts` opens a hidden iframe with `prompt=none`, listens for `oauth-callback` postMessage from `OAuthCallbackPage`, exchanges code → tokens silently. Wired into `requestAccessToken` between `attemptSilentRefresh` (returning users) and `performPopupAuth` (interactive fallback). Marketplace-installed users with pre-granted scopes get zero second-prompt; direct users see no behavioral change (Google returns `login_required` → falls through). `OAuthCallbackPage` extended to handle iframe mode (posts to `window.parent` when no opener but inside iframe). For issue #2: added 'Don't have the password?' disclosure on `LoadPodView`'s password unlock screen explaining the user needs an invite link from the family owner. NO redirect to `/create` — not a valid path for someone trying to open an existing pod. **(4) Auth pages allowlist for /open + /create** — `f985ec6` fixes a regression surfaced in greg's incognito test: hitting `app.beanies.family/open?state=...` flashed loading then redirected to `/welcome`. Root cause: App.vue's runtime `authPages` allowlist hardcoded `['Welcome', 'Login', 'JoinFamily']` and route-name guard ran BEFORE the page could parse state. `meta.requiresAuth: false` is per-route but App.vue's runtime check uses `route.name` not meta. Added `'OpenFromDrive'` and `'CreateFamily'` to the allowlist. **(5) Disclosure refinement (frontend-design pass)** — `ddeaec8` revised the 'no password?' UX after greg flagged v1 as too cookie-cutter. v1 was generic SaaS info-card (ⓘ icon in soft blue panel). v2 mirrors the brand's existing security-card vocabulary already in this view: white squircle (`rounded-[18px]`) + brand soft-shadow + Sky Silk icon-circle. Key icon (semantic match for 'no password = no key') instead of generic info-circle. Reads as part of the same visual system. **Dependabot wave** — triaged 8 open dep PRs at session start. Merged #192 (dev-deps grouped patch bumps, all 12 checks green); closed #199 astro 5→6, #198 vue-router 4→5, #197 stylelint 16→17, #196 eslint 9→10, #195 @astrojs/mdx 4→5, #194 vite 7→8, #193 typescript 5→6 with explanatory comments per-PR (peer-dep blockers vs needs-focused-migration). All major bumps deferred per existing engineering-followups plan. **Marketplace resubmit ops** — greg sent yesterday's drafted reply to `gwm-review@google.com` early in session (DONE); cancelled the Marketplace SDK submission to update the description (entered DRAFT status); updated Drive UI Integration Console (Default MIME Types: `application/octet-stream`, Default File Extensions: `beanpod`, Open URL: `https://app.beanies.family/open`); removed `userinfo.profile` from declared OAuth scopes (we only request `userinfo.email` at runtime, the extra scope was a mismatch flagged in Google's review). > **Earlier 2026-05-05 update** (Tuesday — long session, 14 commits, four Vue prod deploys, end-to-end across cleanup → UX polish → Marketplace integration → production-bug-from-Slack-alert. **(1) Cleanup wave** — `003c1b6` added `tfplan.out` to `.gitignore` (was missing alongside `*.tfplan`) and dropped the dead `UserFamilyMapping.familyRole` field from `src/types/models.ts` + the two write sites in `authStore.ts:227,400` (never read anywhere; existing IDB rows keep their stale value harmlessly, no migration needed since IDB has no index on the field). `5ffc570` resolved the brand-alert-color tension across `CLAUDE.md` + `.claude/skills/beanies-theme/SKILL.md` + `docs/brand/beanies-cig-v2.html` — the headline rule "never use Alert Red" was internally contradicted by the existing carve-out one line below it ("Keep `red-*` for destructive actions and hard form-validation errors"); decision (a) was taken (update docs to reflect actual practice) rather than (b) (migrate `BaseButton.danger` + `ConfirmModal.danger` + `PhotoViewer` red borders to a Heritage Orange variant). Heritage Orange remains the colour for routine alerts (warnings, budget overruns, missed targets, notifications); red is now explicitly scoped to destructive confirmations + hard validation errors only. **(2) Activity-categories wave** — `f287c20` added a new "Food" group to activity categories with 6 items (Brunch 🥞, Coffee ☕, Dining Out 🍽️, Drinks 🍹, Picnic 🧺, Other Food 🍴) using cyan/teal palette matching the transaction-side Food group; mappings to expense categories went into `activityCategoryToExpenseCategory()` so logging "Friday brunch" pre-fills `dining_out`. `cc9cb56` then aligned three sources of truth that had drifted — `ACTIVITY_CATEGORIES` runtime list, `ActivityCategory` type union, and `activityCategoryToExpenseCategory` map. The 2026-05-03 Entertainment ship had landed in (1) and (3) but missed (2); today's Food ship caught `field_trip` missing from (3); Sports `mma`+`taekwondo` had also drifted from the type union. Fix: 11 missing IDs added to the type union, `field_trip → school_fees` added to the mapping, and a new structural-invariant test (`src/constants/__tests__/activityCategories.test.ts`, 4 tests) parses all three sources and asserts the ID sets match in both directions — locks the regression so future drift fails CI. **(3) Notion-canonical guide sync** — `6ed3d78` synced four typo fixes from greg's Notion edits to `content/guides/family-organization.md` (Notion is canonical per `feedback_content_notion_canonical` memory). Diff filtered out Notion-side render artifacts (auto-converted absolute URLs from relative paths, auto-linked bare "beanies.family" text, numbered-list rendering quirk where Notion stores each item as `1.`) — only deliberate prose edits applied. Bumped `lastUpdated` to 2026-05-05. **(4) Calendar swipe gesture** — `5f6d493` added a generic `useHorizontalSwipe` composable using Pointer Events (uniform across touch / pen / mouse drag for both PWA + browser), with three guardrails: axis-lock (only commits if first ~8px is horizontal, |dx| > 1.5×|dy|), iOS edge-back avoidance (touches starting <20px from left edge ignored on touch only — mouse drags from edge still fire), and 60px commit threshold. Wired into all three calendar views — `DailyCalendarView` (swipe → prev/next day), `WeeklyCalendarView` (prev/next week), `CalendarGrid` (prev/next month) — with `touch-action: pan-y` on each swipe target so the browser doesn't pre-empt horizontal pans. 14 unit tests covering direction mapping, threshold respect, axis lock, edge ignore, pointer cancel, progress callback, and the reactive enabled flag. **(5) Drive "Open with beanies.family" integration** — Google Workspace Marketplace SDK enabled mid-session for the listing's `.beanpod` Open URL handler. `3b42364` added `OpenFromDrivePage.vue` at `/open` route — parses the `state` JSON Drive sends (`{"ids":["FILE_ID"],"action":"open","userId":"..."}`), calls `syncStore.loadFromGoogleDrive(fileId, ...)`, and routes to `/login` with the encrypted envelope already loaded so `LoadPodView` jumps straight to password decrypt (no file picker, no second OAuth). Pure parser extracted to `src/utils/driveOpenState.ts` with 12 unit tests. `LoginPage.onMounted` extended to detect pre-loaded `pendingEncryptedFile` and skip welcome → load-pod with `autoLoadPod=true`. `2aba11d` updated privacy.astro + terms.astro to fix three accuracy gaps Marketplace review would have flagged — removed false "app-specific folder" claim (drive.file is per-file, not folder-scoped), corrected OAuth scope description (we only request `userinfo.email`, not name/picture), added new "google workspace marketplace" section explaining the "Open with" gesture, plus explicit `.beanpod` content disclosure. `969cb84` fixed a popup-blocker race surfaced by direct-entry testing — `loadFromGoogleDrive` calls `requestAccessToken` which may open an OAuth popup; popups fired automatically on page load (no user gesture) get blocked. Added a 'popup-blocked' status with a Continue button that retries in click context (gesture satisfies the popup blocker). Genuine Drive flow won't hit this because Drive auto-OAuth pre-warms the grant; this is for direct-entry / bookmarked URLs. `88a6fc0` then tightened the /open recovery UI — previous side-by-side equal-weight buttons ("Sign in directly" + "Continue") were misleading. Restructured to canonical beanies CTA pattern: ONE full-width Heritage Orange gradient primary button + small text-link escape hatch underneath. Labels clarified: "Continue" → "Continue with Google" (names the destination), "Sign in directly" → "or pick a different pod →". Marketplace listing was submitted for review mid-session; first review email landed late evening requesting test creds + screen recording. **(6) Combobox UX fix** — `94993d2` (deploy-skipped earlier, deployed today) added optional `rich` field to `ComboboxOption` that lets consumers pass structured `primary` + `secondary` + `badge` text; when present, the dropdown renders two-line items with a right-aligned monospace badge — Google Flights / Kayak pattern. Wired airports (city primary, airport name secondary, IATA code badge) and cruise ports (city primary, port-name + country secondary, no badge). Search still runs against full `label` so typing "Changi" or "JFK" or "Heathrow" all match. Backward compat: every other combobox consumer keeps single-line layout. **(7) Add another activity** — `4483ebc` added a small "+ add another activity" text-link below the OK button on the activity-created confirmation modal; reduces friction for batch-create flows (kid's term-of-school routine, vacation-week setup) where parents typically add 4-6 activities back-to-back. CreatedConfirmModal got optional `allowCreateAnother` prop + `create-another` emit; FamilyPlannerPage tracks `lastCreatedDate` and on the emit closes the confirm modal + reopens `openAddModal(date)` pre-filled with the same date. Time and assignee deliberately NOT carried forward (different per activity in real batch flows). **(8) Production bug fix from `#beanies-errors`** — `6cf540d` fixed an iOS Safari user (Xu Family / Sejal) hitting `null is not an object (evaluating 't.value.id')` in `ActivityViewEditModal.saveDraft`. Triaged via `/error-review`: `activity` is a computed ref returning null when `props.activity` becomes null; `saveDraft` awaits `chooseScope()` and store mutations, and post-await `activity.value.id` accesses raced the prop nullification (modal dismiss / navigation / Drive sync delete mid-save). Fix: capture `editing = activity.value` once at function entry, use stable local for all 14 internal accesses. Side benefit — edits also persist correctly even if user dismisses the modal mid-save (captured ID is still valid in IndexedDB). Sibling check: TodoViewEditModal + TransactionViewEditModal have similar saveDraft shape but only ONE terminal `await` with `.value.id` read synchronously into the arg before the suspend — race doesn't exist there. **(9) E2E webkit flake harden** — `9edbbb6` bumped `AccountsPage.selectAccountType` subtype waitFor from 15s → 30s to absorb tail latency on `cross-entity.spec.ts:379` (Loan & Activity Linking) which has hard-failed across the last 4 webkit-CI runs. Same flake family hardened twice already (2026-04-30 v-if→v-show in AccountCategoryPicker, 2026-05-03 modal close-then-open ordering). Underlying picker fix is in place; residual issue is webkit-CI's tail latency on the `display:none` flip + a11y-tree visibility recompute. Logged the recurrence in `docs/E2E_HEALTH.md` with a concrete next-step plan if 30s recurs (poll picker's collapse-state signal before subtype, OR quarantine on webkit). **Marketplace listing journey**: greg enabled the Google Workspace Marketplace SDK + Drive integration mid-day, configured Drive UI Integration with `.beanpod` extension, `application/octet-stream` MIME, Open URL `/open`, auto-OAuth checkbox, Public visibility, `Drive app` integration framework. Tested live — desktop Drive doesn't yet show "Open with beanies.family" (review-gated), mobile Drive shows it but click is no-op (cached registration without backend activation). Listing submitted for review; Google's `gwm-review@google.com` replied late evening asking for test credentials + sample `.beanpod` shared with `gsmtestuser@marketplacetest.net` + screen recording demonstrating the requested scopes — drafted the reply in conversation, deferred to greg to send tomorrow. **Aloe Vera launch — Day 5 of 14**: Tuesday LinkedIn post went live (greg's first beanies-related LinkedIn). KiKi Walter (The Memoirist Substack) replied to the Sunday DM, accepted the piece, posted a link to it in her feed — brought a few new likes + Substack subscribers. Greg deferred the Deb Harman / Wind Phone Medium reply for 1-2 weeks pending Audacity / Narratively / Longreads decisions (since publishing on Medium would burn the reprint angle for those reprint-silent pubs). Mother's Day amplification weekend (May 10-11) still ahead. **Test status throughout**: 1949 → 1979 unit tests passing (+30 from useHorizontalSwipe + activityCategories invariant + driveOpenState parser); type-check + lint + build clean across all 14 commits. **Other-terminal commits**: none today.)

> **Earlier 2026-05-03 update (Sunday late-night — three-fix follow-up session triggered by greg pasting a Slack `#beanies-errors` alert from his Android Chrome PWA: `Failed to fetch dynamically imported module: .../FamilyNookPage-DZK3iJ0T.js` while opening the app on mobile (desktop unaffected). Three Vue prod deploys spread across the session; final commit `df395df`. **(1) PWA self-heal for stale-chunk crashes after deploy** (commit `b19a106`, deploy run `25272066664`, build 1m6s + deploy 22s): pre-existing structural bug surfaced for the first time. The PWA's workbox precaches `index.html` per `vite-plugin-pwa` config, and that HTML references content-hashed chunk filenames (e.g. `FamilyNookPage-DZK3iJ0T.js`). After every prod deploy those filenames rotate; users still on a previously-precached `index.html` 404 on every lazy `import()` → "oh no, the beans spilled" overlay → the overlay's "Reload" button was a soft `window.location.reload()` that the SW just intercepted and re-served the same stale HTML, leaving users looping until the SW updated on its own (often hours, depending on `registerType: 'prompt'` + when the user dismissed/accepted the update banner). Five-part fix in one PR: new `src/utils/hardReload.ts` recovery primitive (SW `update()` → `SKIP_WAITING` → `caches.delete()` for all caches → `location.replace()`); `router.onError` matches chunk-load message shapes (Chrome / Firefox / Safari variants) and calls `hardReload()` automatically with a `sessionStorage` loop guard cleared by `afterEach` so future deploys can recover too; `vite:preloadError` window listener catches the modulepreload variant; chunk-load failures filtered out of `unhandledrejection` so they stop spamming `#beanies-errors`; error-overlay Reload + Sign Out & Clear Data both call `hardReload()` instead of soft `location.reload()`; AppHeader refresh icon surfaces "A new version is ready / Reload Now" action toast when `safeServiceWorkerUpdate` detects a waiting SW worker (kept the routine refresh path data-only, didn't promote the icon to always-hard-reload). New `Known noise patterns` memory entry documents the now-filtered signature so future triages start from the known list. **(2) Auth-transient toast suppression** (commit `e74f6b5`, deploy run `25276899596`, build 1m11s + deploy 21s): caught after the first deploy when greg tested the reload-icon flow with an expired Google token + failed silent refresh. Two layers were producing competing user-facing signals for the same auth-transient state — the auth layer (per the recent `e5fee92` self-heal fix) intentionally stays quiet on transient silent-refresh failures and only escalates to a Google reconnect banner on `invalid_grant`, but the sync layer was setting `backgroundSyncError = 'Could not refresh data from cloud'` on every failed read, which `BackgroundSyncBar`'s watcher promoted to a loud warning toast. Result: every reload-icon tap (and every auto-poll cycle) fired the toast even though the auth layer was correctly handling the situation. Classified `backgroundSyncError` by kind (`auth-transient` / `decrypt` / `network`) — sync state still flips so success-toast gating in `AppHeader.handleRefreshAll` works (no false success), but `BackgroundSyncBar` skips the toast when kind is `auth-transient`. Auth layer keeps owning the user-facing escalation. Detection regex matches the `silent refresh failed` substring shared by both `TokenExpiredError` shapes. **(3) Activity modal-stack fix** (commit `df395df`, deploy run `25276899596` — same workflow as #2): caught after E2E ran on the second deploy push. The recurring webkit-only "Activity with monthly fee creates recurring item" flake hit again (3rd hard-fail recurrence — 2026-04-16 / 04-22 / 04-30 / 05-03). Documented in `E2E_HEALTH.md` as `(c) flake` three times running but actually a real ordering bug all along. Root cause finally found: in `FamilyPlannerPage.handleSave`, the create branch opened `CreatedConfirmModal` BEFORE closing `ActivityModal` — so for one tick both `<BaseModal>` instances were in the DOM with `role="dialog"` + `aria-modal="true"`. WebKit's a11y/focus engine, under CI contention, would stall the second modal's visibility for >15s while the first was still teleported in. Reordered: `showModal.value = false` first, `await nextTick()` to let Vue flush the v-if removal, then open the confirm dialog. `E2E_HEALTH.md` updated with the diagnosis + reclassified the prior three (c) entries as the same latent ordering bug (`(a) → (b)`). `TransactionsPage.handleTransactionSave` follows the same close-then-open pattern but without `nextTick`; left as-is since it hasn't flaked, apply the same pattern preemptively if it surfaces. **E2E follow-up — webkit AccountCategoryPicker subtype rendering** (NOT yet fixed, see Pending block): the modal-stack fix removed the symptom that test was failing on but exposed a different webkit-only blocker earlier in the same flow — `cross-entity.spec.ts:347` now hard-fails at `AccountsPage.selectAccountType('checking')` waiting for the `🏦 Checking` subtype button to render. Same shape as 2026-04-30 (`AccountCategoryPicker.vue:126` conditional `v-if="expandedCat()!.subtypes"` lags on webkit-CI under contention). Three other webkit tests passed on retry as flakes hitting the same subtype path. Chromium 21/21 green throughout. Deploy gate (CI + Security) is green so this doesn't block prod — but the activity-monthly-fee test will keep hard-failing on webkit until the v-if is restructured. Suggested fix shape: replace conditional v-if rendering with always-rendered + `v-show`, OR pre-mount the subtype group via `<KeepAlive>`. **Tests/build status**: 1932 unit tests pass throughout the session (was 1920 → +12 from the inter-session owner-role ship that landed mid-session); type-check + lint clean; build clean. **Other-terminal commits merged via rebase\*\*: greg made three commits on a parallel session (`602fcbf` activity categories tidy, `bb5e674` owner role refactor, `3f881a3` evening status wrap) — both my pushes hit fetch-first rebases; CHANGELOG.md merge conflict resolved both times by keeping all entries.)

> **Earlier 2026-05-03 update (Sunday evening):** two ships in one Vue prod deploy (activity categories tidy + owner role refactor with self-heal + transfer ownership flow). **Activity categories tidy** (commit `602fcbf`): triggered by greg flagging that the Fun group label didn't fit its contents (birthday, wedding, bar mitzvah, other celebration are parties, not "fun" generically) and that no Entertainment category existed for movies/shows/concerts. Renamed `Fun` → `Party` (group label only, contents unchanged); added new `Entertainment` group with 8 items — Movie 🎬, Show / Musical 🎭, Concert 🎵, Theme Park 🎢, Sporting Event 🏟️, Museum 🏛️, Festival / Fair 🎪, Other Entertainment ✨ — using a rose/pink palette distinct from existing groups. All eight new entertainment activities map to the existing `entertainment` expense category in `activityCategoryToExpenseCategory()` so logging "$45 for Friday movie" pre-fills the right expense bucket. Travel-banner copy at the top of the activity drawer also tightened: `vacation.planningATrip` "Make It a Family Vacation!" → "Add a Travel Plan!" (closer match to what tapping it actually does — opens the trip planner, not commits to a vacation). 11 translation keys updated/added; `npm run translate` regenerated Chinese cleanly with 1 stale key removed. **Owner role refactor end-to-end** (commit `bb5e674`): triggered by greg looking at his prod pod (Parker Meng Beanies, one of the earliest prod families) and seeing zero Owner pills on any card — confirmed via the chip text he could read off the cards: he was "Admin", everyone else "Member", nobody had `role === 'owner'`. Three connected problems addressed in one PR per CLAUDE.md sustainability principles (DRY/elegance/no-silent-failures/multi-pass review). **(1) Missing-Owner self-heal**: `applyDefaults()` in `familyMemberRepository.ts` was backfilling six fields but not `role`, so legacy/migrated records with a stripped owner had no defensive heal — `role` came back as `undefined`, the `currentRole !== 'owner'` check trivially passed, and the dropdown rendered "Member" instead of the Owner pill. Fix: `applyDefaults()` now backfills `role: member.role ?? 'member'`, and a new `normalizeRoles()` helper runs on every load (idempotent, single `changeDoc()` per write batch) — ensures exactly one Owner exists by promoting the earliest-created human (preferring the `requiresPassword === false` family-creator seat) if none, demoting later owners if multiple, and migrating legacy `admin` rows to `member` while persisting `canManagePod = true` so existing admins keep their effective permission survives the rule change. canManagePod default in applyDefaults narrowed from `(role === 'owner' || role === 'admin')` to `(role === 'owner')` only — safe because normalize stamps the flag explicitly before the rule has effect. Fast-path early return when no writes are needed (no-op on subsequent loads). reportError fires on the no-owner-promoted case so we capture how often the heal triggers in the wild. **(2) Card cleanup**: `MemberRoleManager.vue` deleted entirely (37 LOC + the only consumer was BeanCard.vue:250). Owner identity is now a Heritage Orange 👑 crown overlaid on the owner's avatar (top-right corner, 36% size, white ring, mirrors BeanieAvatar's existing bottom-right filter-badge mechanics — single additive `ownerBadge?: boolean` prop with a code comment explaining the slot/prop evolution path when a second per-avatar badge is needed). Non-owner cards show no role chip at all — frees the name row entirely. Granular permission editing (canManagePod / canViewFinances / canEditActivities) is unchanged: it still lives in the existing pencil-icon `FamilyMemberModal.vue`, where it always was. Dead `updateMemberRole()` action also removed from familyStore (no callers after Stage 2). **(3) Transfer Pod Ownership flow**: new Settings → Transfer Pod Ownership tile (gated to `isOwner === true`) opens a three-step `BeanieFormModal` — single host modal, no nested overlay stacking. Step 1 picks an adult human via `<FamilyChipPicker>` (extended with one additive `members?: FamilyMember[]` prop so caller can pass an adults-minus-current-owner list without coupling the picker to new filter logic). Step 2 re-authenticates via new `<ReauthChallenge>` component at `src/components/auth/` — passkey-first via `authenticateWithPasskey()`, falls back to `<PasswordModal>` with `verifyPassword()` against the owner's stored hash. Edge case (no passkey + no password) shows a clear "Set a password in Settings → Security first" recovery prompt and reports the case so we know if it ever fires. Cancellation drops back to choice (silent — passkey-cancellation is established noise per ADR). Step 3 confirms with danger-styled "Transfer Ownership" button. The store action `transferOwnership(toMemberId)` does the demote + promote in a **single atomic `changeDoc()` callback** so Automerge sees one transaction (no partial-failure reconciliation code needed). In-place `members.value.map(...)` update follows the existing `updateMember()` pattern (faster than full re-fetch + re-walk through applyDefaults). New `authStore.updateCurrentUserRole()` keeps the session role in sync if the outgoing or incoming owner is the current authenticated user — UI re-renders with the new state without a reload. Registry `UserFamilyMapping.familyRole` write **deliberately skipped**: `grep -rn familyRole` confirmed it's written at signup but never read anywhere — writing dead data adds complexity for zero gain; tracked as separate cleanup tech debt. `<ReauthChallenge>` extracted as reusable since three foreseeable callers exist (transfer ownership, future delete-pod, future leave-pod) — clear contract (`open` + `member` props, `verified`/`cancelled` events). 12 new unit tests cover normalizeRoles (one-owner no-op, two-owners demote, no-owner promote-creator, no-owner promote-fallback, admin migration with canManagePod preserved, changeDoc throw resilience, empty list) and transferOwnership (happy path, pet target rejection, same-owner rejection, ghost target rejection, authStore role sync). All 1932 unit tests pass; type-check + lint clean. **Plan archived**: `docs/plans/2026-05-03-owner-role-refactor.md` — multi-pass review applied (DRY pass identified `<ReauthChallenge>` extraction + `members?` prop reuse + `BeanieAvatar.ownerBadge` prop reuse over building new components; sustainability pass killed nested-modal stacking design + `useConfirm` overlay + dead-code carry; correctness pass replaced full re-fetch with in-place map). **Both ships deployed in one Vue prod workflow** (run `25273999158`, build 1m4s + deploy 22s) via `/deploy-prod-auto-skip-ci` — `classify-changes.sh` saw both `602fcbf` and `bb5e674` as pending since the morning's `e74f6b5` deploy. CI/Security workflows triggered on push are running in the background (not awaited).

> **Earlier 2026-05-01 update (evening):** auth-reliability follow-up session, two deploys, two surgical fixes that close real symptoms greg hit in production. **Picker error disambiguation + Slack alerting for all join-flow errors** (commit `9360701`, Vue prod deploy `25218765602`, build 1m8s + deploy 22s): triggered by greg testing onboarding for a stuck user (Xu Family / `elaine1.galao@gmail.com` on iPhone iOS 18.7 / Safari 26.3) — the user kept hitting `PICKER_SCRIPT_LOAD_FAILED` after successful OAuth, and greg reproduced the _same_ error on his Android Chrome 147 by scanning her invite link and signing in to her Google account on consent. Audit found three connected issues: (1) the `reason: 'script'` value was overloaded across 5 distinct underlying failure modes — config missing, script-tag load fail, picker construction throw (most likely "google.picker is undefined" post-load), auth-chain throw, plus the existing iframe/timeout paths — all collapsed to one opaque code with the `(e as Error).message` logged to console and dropped on the floor. (2) Worse, picker errors _never reached `#beanies-errors`_ — only 1 of 8 `currentError.value = ...` sites in `useJoinFlow.ts` called `reportError`, and it wasn't the picker one. We'd been debugging from user-copy-pasted diagnostic blobs the whole time. (3) The `scriptPromise` was cached at module scope and never reset on `onerror`, so the "Retry" button on a failed picker called `loadPickerScript` → returned the same rejected promise forever without ever attempting another fetch. Fix shipped: widened `PickBeanpodFileResult.failed.reason` to `'config' | 'load' | 'open' | 'auth' | 'iframe' | 'timeout'` with an optional `message` carrying the captured exception text through to the diagnostic blob; new `recordError` helper in `useJoinFlow` consolidates all 8 error sites into one source of truth that ALWAYS fires `reportError` with the captured detail in the message body; `scriptPromise = null` reset on `script.onerror`; force-consent on the `'needs-pick'` fallthrough path in `runCloudFlow` so the picker doesn't silently re-use a wrong-account silent token; structural cleanup of `usePickBeanpodFile.pick`'s try-block to narrow it to only the auth chain (since `pickBeanpodFile` always-resolves by contract — making the auth-vs-picker boundary visible in code prevents future drift). The 5-reason taxonomy maps cleanly onto the existing 3 picker codes (no new error codes, no new i18n strings, no new components, no view changes). 1908 → 1916 unit tests pass; type-check + lint clean. **Silent Google token refresh self-healing across reload + tab wake** (commit `e5fee92`, Vue prod deploy `25220481552`, build 1m + deploy 22s): triggered by greg seeing the yellow reconnect toast 3× today after multi-hour inactivity AND right after tapping the "get fresh beans" banner — every reconnect succeeded via the manual flow, proving the refresh token in IndexedDB was valid and the system just wasn't doing the silent refresh it should have. Three-track audit (`expiryCallbacks` trigger chain, refresh-token persistence/restore, Drive-sync auth + 401 handling) found five contributing issues, all addressed in one fix: (1) `getValidTokenSilent()` was firing the banner from inside its own catch path on ANY silent-refresh failure including transients (network blip, 5xx, mid-SW activation), so callers had no chance to retry before the user saw it. (2) Module-level `setTimeout` for auto-refresh dies on full page reload AND is throttled while the tab is hidden — no `visibilitychange` listener proactively re-validated the token on focus. (3) Banner had no self-healing path — once up, stayed up even when a later silent refresh succeeded. (4) Single-attempt wake refresh in `attemptSilentReconnect`. (5) Already-correct `invalid_grant` short-circuit (kept). Fix: split callbacks into expiry (transient-eligible, no banner) vs `onTokenPermanentlyExpired` (`invalid_grant` only — the banner's sole legitimate trigger); new `installAuthWakeListener` registered from `initializeAuth` proactively calls `attemptSilentRefresh` on `visibilitychange` AND once at install when the token expires within ~2 minutes (covers cold boot / SW reload); `syncStore.setupTokenExpiryHandler` now subscribes `handleGoogleReconnected` to `onTokenAcquired` so any successful silent refresh auto-clears the banner; `MAX_ATTEMPTS` bumped 2 → 3 with stepped backoff `[1500, 3000]` ms. Net effect: the toast appears only when Google has actually revoked the refresh token (`invalid_grant`), never on transient failures, tab-wake, or SW reload. New `__resetWakeListenerForTesting` export prevents stale-listener accumulation across `vi.resetModules()` boundaries. 1908 → 1916 unit tests pass; type-check + lint clean. **Plans archived**: `docs/plans/2026-05-01-silent-token-refresh-reliability.md` (today's session). The earlier picker-error plan from this morning is preserved in the `in-the-past-we-compressed-spindle.md` ephemeral plan-mode file (not migrated to `docs/plans/` because the deploy skill needed plan-mode-exit timing that interfered with the canonical archive flow). **What to watch over the next 24-48h**: `#beanies-errors` channel for any new `join-flow:PICKER_*` reports — they'll now carry the captured exception text in the Slack message body, giving us the input for any Phase 2 targeted fix (most likely candidate: `picker_construct` with `google.picker is undefined` → add a post-condition check + one-shot retry in `loadPickerLibrary`). Greg should also see the reconnect toast disappear from his daily experience unless his Google account actually revokes the grant.)
