Also discovered `@fontsource-variable/outfit` + `inter` weren't installed in workspace at start of session (ran `npm install` to fix a local-dev error). **Previous update (2026-04-22, afternoon):** Claude (afternoon session covered — 2 commits on `main`, no prod deploys. **(1) E2E webkit flake fix** (`ddd7bb1`): three webkit tests hard-failing/flaking on CI because the inline `dismissCreatedConfirm` helper used a 5s click timeout on the CreatedConfirmModal's OK button — trace showed the "Add Activity" save click succeeded but the confirm modal rendered later than the window allowed on webkit under contention. Extracted `dismissActivityCreatedConfirm` to new shared helper `e2e/helpers/activity-modal.ts` that waits for the "Activity Created" heading first (15s) as a semantic signal, then clicks OK. Deduplicates the inline helper that was copy-pasted across `planner.spec.ts` (4 call sites) and `cross-entity.spec.ts` (2 call sites). CI run 24758643613 confirmed: webkit 19/21 passed + 2 unrelated flakes that passed on retry; chromium 21/21 clean. E2E_HEALTH.md logged as (c) flake with fix rationale. **(2) 4 pillar guides authored** (Notion → repo sync, Notion is golden source going forward): all 4 marketing guides now live in `content/guides/` marked `draft: true`. **Pillar 1 (Overwhelmed with Family Planning)** already in repo — bumped `lastUpdated` + `buy-fruit` already in relatedPosts. **Pillar 2 (Family Organization)** new — ~3.1k words, 10 H2s with `short answer:` blocks, 4 typos fixed, 5 links inserted (overwhelmed guide ×2, travel-plans-intro blog, Four Thousand Weeks by Burkeman), 1 pending link to the cozi-alternatives blog post (placeholder + HTML comment; greg adds Friday when that post ships). **Pillar 3 (Family Finance Basics)** new — ~3.6k words, 11 H2s with `short answer:` blocks, 9 typos fixed, YMYL callout added as first body element ("not financial advice, consult a professional"), DTI thresholds softened and cited to CFPB's ability-to-repay rule, salary→expenses inconsistency fixed in the emergency-fund section, $10/mo compounding caveat added (nominal vs invested at 7%). **Pillar 4 (Local-first Family Tools)** new — ~3.2k words, 10 H2s with `short answer:` blocks, 6 typos fixed (incl. Automerge capitalization), 14 external link insertions (Mint sunset announcement, Sunrise TechCrunch, The Markup Life360 investigation, Ink & Switch ×2, Automerge ×2, Yjs, MDN: Web Crypto + IndexedDB + File System Access, Tauri, Capacitor, Cozi/Time Inc acquisition PR); family-app-shutdowns sentence rewritten to link both Sunrise and Mint. Link format is `[anchor](URL) ← <annotation> <!-- link added: greg confirm -->` — annotation preserved for greg's audit grep. **Notion bidirectional sync** — ~49 Notion block updates + 3 archives + 1 new callout block (Pillar 3 YMYL) to mirror the repo versions. Notion page title/properties untouched; STATUS/TARGET/PROPOSED FRONTMATTER working notes left intact. **Preview verified**: all 4 URLs return 200 on `npm run dev:web`; greg visually confirmed pages look good. Residual cleanup flagged for next session (arrow artifacts from Notion still in the repo markdown). **Previous update (2026-04-22, morning)**: 1 commit deployed to prod (`68186cf`). **Net-worth chart correctness + aggregation hygiene fix.** Five bugs (one user-reported, four latent) addressed in a single PR with comprehensive plan + test coverage. **Bug A — balance_adjustment in chart history** (the reported bug): `useNetWorthHistory` replay loop was silently skipping `balance_adjustment` transactions, so greg's +$500k manual adjustment yesterday rendered as a flat line at the new amount. Now correctly subtracts the adjustment when walking past it (with sign flipped via `accountNetWorthMultiplier` for liability accounts). **Bug B — Reports Income vs Expenses chart** (`ReportsPage.vue:444-462`): the per-tx loop classified every non-income transaction as an expense via a binary ternary, so transfers and balance adjustments rolled into `totalExpenses` / `netCashFlow`. Fixed with an early-continue guard. **Bug C — loan-payment principal in chart**: chart reversed the cash side of loan payments but not the corresponding debt-balance reduction; historical net worth was overstated by cumulative principal paid. Fixed by extending the expense branch to also reverse `loanPrincipalPortion`. **Bug 5 — `createdAt` clamping**: new accounts/assets created today appeared at their current value across the entire historical chart. Now clamped: pre-creation chart points correctly omit the entity. Asset+linked-loan combos cancel correctly to net-zero retroactive contribution. **Bug D — visual misclassification**: NookRecentActivity and GlobalSearch rendered `balance_adjustment` rows with the credit-card icon. Now use a dedicated ⚖️ icon with green/orange tint reflecting direction; transfers get 🔄 with blue tint. **Architecture refactor**: replay logic extracted to a pure `utils/netWorthHistory.ts` module built around a single `NetWorthChange` event abstraction. The composable becomes a thin orchestrator (~50 lines) with a try/catch chartResult wrapper exposing `chartError` to the hero card (3-way flat template: error / empty / chart). **DRY wins**: 6 sites of duplicated `credit_card || loan ? -1 : 1` multiplier logic eliminated via new `finance.ts` helpers (`isLiabilityType`, `accountNetWorthMultiplier`, `accountBalanceDeltaFromTx`); 2 local `isLiability(type)` definitions in DashboardPage + AccountsPage deleted. Sibling `getTransactionVisual` helper in `transactionLabel.ts` so any activity-row surface gets stable icons. Cash-flow computeds extracted out of `useNetWorthHistory` into new `useMonthOverMonthCashFlow` composable so the chart composable stays single-responsibility. **Safety**: `assertNever` (new `utils/assertNever.ts`) on every `tx.type` switch — compile-time exhaustiveness against future TransactionType additions. Warn-and-skip for data anomalies (missing `adjustment` metadata, missing `createdAt`, account not in store). Architectural non-goals doc-block in `netWorthHistory.ts` flags daily-snapshots as the only path forward for asset re-valuation history. Plan: `docs/plans/2026-04-22-net-worth-chart-and-aggregate-correctness.md`. **Tests**: 1339 → 1420 (+81 new tests across `finance.test.ts`, `transactionLabel.test.ts`, new `netWorthHistory.test.ts`, new `useMonthOverMonthCashFlow.test.ts`, new `useNetWorthHistory.test.ts`). 21 files changed, 2258 insertions, 302 deletions. CI + Security green; Vue deploy run `24755152864` succeeded in 1m26s.

Primary footer action is a new `GoalContributionModal` (centered modal, stacks over the drawer) — amount + optional note; crossing 25/50/75/100% of target fires `celebrate('goal-milestone')`; success toast carries an Undo action via existing `useToast.actionFn` (6s); manual rows gain an inline trash button for persistent delete with danger confirm. Manual contributions store inline as `Goal.manualContributions?: GoalManualContribution[] = {id, amount, at, updatedBy, note?}`. Store-level invariant inside `goalsStore.updateGoal(id, input, options?)` — optional `{contribution: {author, note?}}` appends atomically via `appendContributionIfChanged` helper; automated paths (`transactionsStore.applyGoalAllocation`, `recurringProcessor.updateGoalProgress`) omit the options and never emit audit entries. `GoalModal` edit field relabeled "Current amount" → "Remaining amount" with a two-way computed bridge (reducing remaining = positive currentAmount delta = positive contribution). New composables: `useAuthoringMember()` (centralizes `currentMember ?? owner` + no-author toast; `useAdjustBalance` refactored to use it), `useContributeToGoal()` (contribute + saveWithContribution + undoContribution). New shared `<EntityActivityLog>` takes a generic `ActivityEntry[]` with optional `onClick`/`onDelete` per row; fixed-width trailing slot on every row so amounts column-align even when some rows have delete buttons and others don't. Sort within a date group uses full-timestamp tiebreaker (`tx.createdAt` / `contribution.at`) so a just-added contribution appears at the top of today's group. `GoalsPage` card-tap flipped to `openViewModal`; ✏️ icon still opens edit; new `viewingGoalId`/`contributingToGoalId` refs (ids only, with live computeds) so the view modal stays reactive when the store updates the goal reference. `getPriorityConfig` extracted to `src/constants/goalDisplay.ts`. `transactionsStore.transactionsForGoal(id)` derived getter + `TransactionsPage` `?goal=<id>` URL filter + dismissible pill. Audited all call sites: fixed NookYourBeans, FamilyBeanRow, MeetTheBeansPage grid, FamilyScrapbookPage member-filter chips, PickBeanView login picker — all five were iterating `familyStore.members`/`humans` directly without using the sorted getters. **Pet role label** — "Your Beans" row was showing "Parent" / "Big Bean" for pets because `getRoleLabel` only checked role/ageGroup. Added `dashboard.rolePet` ("Pet Beanie" / "pet beanie") and a shared `getMemberRoleLabel()` helper in useMemberInfo; pet check comes first so `isPet` overrides any ageGroup classification. **BeanListStrip consolidation** — NookYourBeans + FamilyBeanRow were 90% identical and drifted through 3 separate bugs today (pet sort, pet role label, missing photo-url). Extracted to single `<BeanListStrip>` with `labelKey` / `addLabelKey` / `density` props. **🟡 Smoke-test the new calendar swipe animation on real hardware (2026-05-14, NOT deployed):**

- Built locally; type-check / lint / 2213 unit tests / build all green via `npm run validate`. Tests cover direction, damping, animation sequence, lockout, reduced-motion — but **not** hands-on feel.
- Smoke on iPhone Safari + iPhone PWA + iPad Safari + desktop Chrome with mouse-drag: swipe across the day / week / month views, confirm the slide-out / slide-in feels smooth (220 ms out + 320 ms in), the rubber-band past threshold is noticeable but not annoying, the spring-back on a short undecided drag goes back to centre, the prev/next/today buttons still snap (intentional), the existing `touch-action: pan-y` still lets vertical scroll work natively inside the calendar. macOS Safari → System Settings → Accessibility → "Reduce motion" → confirm swipe becomes instant (no drag preview, no animation, same as before). If all good: deploy + flip this entry to "deployed".
- Risk areas if it fails: (a) the `requestAnimationFrame` wait between phase A and phase B is one frame — on a slow device the new content might briefly render in the wrong position; bump to two frames if visible. (b) `transform: translate3d` on the swipe container (the `rounded-3xl bg-white p-5 shadow-[...]` card) — confirm the shadow is GPU-composited cleanly during the slide, doesn't flicker.

**🟡 Smoke-test 2026-05-13 UI ships (deployed — `cd13eda` + `0c6b4ba`):**

- All three are covered by `npm run validate` (type-check / lint / format / 2196 unit tests / build) and the new test files (`useCriticalItems` +9, `useExpandableList` ×6, `ShowMoreToggle` ×5, `SmoothHeight` ×2, `LocalFileSyncWarning` ×5) — but **not** a hands-on browser pass.
- **Daily briefing** (`/nook`): with >5 critical items, "Show all N" expands the box _smoothly_ (the box grows with a 320 ms ease-out, rows 6+ cascade in), "Show less" folds it back to 5; switch the current member (member picker) and check an unassigned to-do shows for everyone ("… (anyone can do this)"), a child-only to-do shows for adults as "{child}: …" and for the child as "Don't forget: …", adding a parent narrows it; pets never show a briefing.
- **Planner**: "Upcoming activities" with >6 items — "View more" pages by 6, "Show less" resets, the list grows/shrinks smoothly. Planner-sidebar to-do preview: same.
- **Onboarding** (Welcome Gate → "Create a new pod" → Step 1 → Step 2): the Google Drive hero card (icon + "Recommended" pill + benefit line + "Connect Google Drive →"), the quiet "Prefer to store your data locally on this device?" link → the reworked modal ("Heads up — local files don't sync", three short lines, "Use Google Drive instead" / "Use a local file" / × top-right) → connecting Drive flips the card green ("Connected to Google Drive") and enables "Next"; picking local turns the link into "✓ Saving to a local file on this device". Dark mode + the "How this works" / "More providers coming soon" disclosures.

**🟡 `#beanies-errors` watch for `app.chunkRecoveryFailed` (post-`d86e797`, deployed 2026-05-13):**

- `App.vue`'s init catch fires this when `isChunkLoadError(err) === true` AND the bounded-retry counter has already exhausted (3 silent hardReloads across all three callsites — `App.vue` catch / `router.onError` / `main.ts:vite:preloadError`). Includes `route_path` + the error message + the `attempts` count in the context. Cleared automatically on a successful boot via App.vue's post-init health check.
- **Baseline expectation: silent in steady state.** If it fires for any user/device, the layered fix (regex, SW unregister, CDN no-cache, old-chunk retention, bounded counter) isn't enough for that device class and we'd want to add another layer (likely: auto-trigger the "Sign out & clear data" path after N attempts instead of just showing the overlay). The very first deploy after a user's last load is the highest-risk window — if it's going to fire, that's where.

**🟡 `app.onboardingZombieState` pattern-watch (passive — no action unless a family stays stuck):**

- Fires ONCE per OAuth-redirect-return per page-load in the zombie state. The single alert per round-trip is expected — it's the safety net working. Pattern signals: (a) the same `family_id` firing multiple times across hours/days → a user is genuinely stuck on the resume-setup screen and may need a nudge; (b) the alert firing with `route_path` other than `/welcome` or `/welcome?resume=setup` → the router guard caught it on an SPA nav (e.g. `/activities`) which means they were trying to use the app while their session was zombie'd. (c) The `family_email` context being null in the Slack message (shown as "(not yet authenticated)") is a minor format oddity — it doesn't mean the user isn't authenticated; just that the family context wasn't initialized when the alert fired. Worth a small follow-up to backfill from `authStore.currentUser.email` if `family_email` is unset.

**🟡 greg's own Dieffenbach-Family device is in the zombie state (caught live 2026-05-13):**

- Per the `app.onboardingZombieState` alert at 11:14:35 UTC: greg's iPhone (iOS 26.3.1, Chrome/CriOS) on family "The Dieffenbach Family · …eddff76a" tried to deep-link into `/activities` while in the authenticated-but-no-pod state. - Smoke list (from `docs/plans/2026-05-12-storage-provider-migration.md` § Verification): (1) local → Drive: onboard fresh on local file, add an account + transaction, Move to Google Drive → confirm; verify the success toast names both files, the badge flips to Drive, the file appears in Drive, "Last Saved" updates on the next edit, the local file is untouched by subsequent edits. (2) Drive → local: symmetric, via the OS save-picker. (3) Cancellation each direction: dismiss the picker / OAuth chooser → silent "Move cancelled" toast, no console error, provider unchanged, Family Data error box stays hidden. (4) Rollback: monkey-patch `syncService.save` (Vue devtools) to throw once, trigger Move, confirm rollback completes with the error toast naming the source, then keep editing → saves still go to the source. (5) **Safari PWA, local → Drive, no cached token**: redirect-auth happens once, page returns to Settings with Drive connected, the user taps Move again, migration completes **without a second redirect** (the `forceConsent:false` fix).
- Also worth a one-off: re-run the existing onboarding/settings E2E specs to confirm the additive owner-gated row didn't disturb them (no new E2E added — needs Drive mocking, would breach the 25-test ADR-007 cap).

**🟡 `#beanies-errors` watch for `storage-migration-*` (post-`3a457e4`, deployed 2026-05-12):**

- `migrateStorage` reports `{surface:'storage-migration-ok', severity:'warning', context:{from,to}}` on success and `{surface:'storage-migration-failed', severity:'error', context:{from,to,step}}` on failure (`step` ∈ `pre-swap`/`install`/`rollback`). The component's failure toasts pass `{silent:true}` so they don't double-report.
- Baseline expectation: occasional `storage-migration-ok` warnings = real adoption (great signal). Zero error-level pings under healthy conditions. A `step:'install'` failure means the move was rolled back cleanly (pod still on the source). A `step:'rollback'` failure means the rollback itself broke — **P1**: the user was told to sign out and back in to recover; check `fileHandleStore.getProviderConfig` state and triage via `/error-review`.

**🟡 prettier-plugin-tailwindcss inline-style line-wrap quirk (residual from CSS cleanup):**

- Surfaced 2026-05-07 in the `eb03538` → `96b7b93` cycle: when an inline `style="..."` attribute is long enough to wrap, the plugin splits it mid-value and produces malformed HTML (literal newlines inside a CSS property). CI's prettier then flags it as a format error. Workaround that day was to drop the redundant inline override on the washi divider (the value was a no-op). Pattern to watch for: any element with both Tailwind classes AND a long inline `style=""` attribute.
- **Out of scope** for the 2026-05-08 CSS structural cleanup per that plan — separate concern (project-level lint rule vs. plugin version pin vs. upstream bug). No urgency unless it bites again.
- Investigation paths (when prioritised): (a) reproduce minimally and file an upstream issue at github.com/tailwindlabs/prettier-plugin-tailwindcss; (b) pin the plugin version in `package.json` to the last known-good if a regression PR landed; (c) add a project-level prettier override that disables the plugin for inline `style` attributes specifically.

**🟡 OAuth-fetch-timeout deploy telemetry watch (post-`2c86108`):**

- Today's deploy added 15s `AbortController`-based timeouts to both `oauthProxy.ts` fetches (`exchangeCodeForTokens` + `refreshAccessToken`). Targeted three Slack alerts of `cold-start-reconnect-escalation` from greg's iPhone PWA in 4 hours plus user-reported frozen "counting beans..." spinner after manual reconnect.
- **Watch `#beanies-errors` for the next 24-72h.** What we expect to see:
  - **Best case:** zero new `cold-start-reconnect-escalation` alerts. Silent refresh succeeds, no banner fires.
  - **Acceptable case:** if any do fire, the message will end "**OAuth proxy fetch timed out after 15000ms**" — clean signal that the proxy/network is the bottleneck (not our code hanging). That would be its own escalation story (Lambda warmup latency? CloudFront issue?) but with fail-fast diagnostics.
  - **Bad case:** alerts fire with the old `lastError: Google access token expired and silent refresh failed` shape (no "timed out" suffix) — would mean the timeout didn't catch and we still have a hung-fetch path somewhere.
- Defense-in-depth: `App.vue:512` wraps `completeRedirectAuth()` in a 20s `Promise.race` so app init can never wedge even if a future code path adds another awaited fetch.
- Greg's testimony "happening much more than before" is fully explained by the 2026-05-08 `8152872` hardening lowering the failure-counter threshold 3 → 2 (faster escalation exposed the underlying hang sooner). The hang itself was always there.
- **No follow-up needed if no alerts fire**; just retire this block at next session's validation pass.

**🟡 Webkit-CI E2E flake watch — `selectAccountType` poll-for-hint (`5beac12`) + `page.goto('/')` navigation-interrupt (`906ce0e`):**

- **`selectAccountType` poll-for-hint (`5beac12`, 2026-05-09)** — replaced the 30s subtype-visibility waitFor with a poll-for-"Select a type"-hint signal (E2E_HEALTH 2026-05-09 entry; 6th recurrence of that flake). Status as of 2026-05-11: **did not recur this session** across multiple push-CI + manual E2E runs. If it does recur at the new poll signal → (a) cull per ADR-007's >2× recurrence threshold, or (b) quarantine on webkit only (chromium gating remains).
- **`page.goto('/')` navigation-interrupt (`906ce0e`, 2026-05-11)** — new flake: 8 webkit tests across 6 spec files failed at once in `beforeEach` hooks with `Navigation to "/" is interrupted by another navigation to "/nook"` (SPA router guard redirects `/` on first paint, racing the default `until: 'load'` wait under CI contention). `gh run rerun --failed` passed clean — pure flake. Hardened: new `e2e/helpers/navigation.ts` `gotoRoot(page)` → `page.goto('/', { waitUntil: 'commit' })`; all 18 bare `page.goto('/')` sites across 5 spec files replaced. Watch the next 2-3 webkit-CI runs: if the harden held, retire this bullet; if a different navigation-interrupt shape shows up (e.g. on `page.goto('/settings')` or `page.reload()`), apply the same `waitUntil: 'commit'` treatment there.
- Both fixes are e2e/-only — no prod impact; the deploy gate doesn't depend on the E2E Tests workflow.

**🟢 ActivityCategory type union — sibling latent drift (small cleanup, no urgency):**

- `cc9cb56` aligned the three sources of truth that had drifted (`ACTIVITY_CATEGORIES` runtime list, `ActivityCategory` type union, `activityCategoryToExpenseCategory` map) and added an invariant test that locks all three together.
- However, `src/constants/activityPresets.ts` references activity IDs as `category: ActivityCategory` literals — these are typed but the file isn't covered by the new invariant test. Future drift in presets won't fail CI until either (a) a preset references a non-existent ID (would be caught by tsc immediately, fine), OR (b) a preset references a stale ID that was removed from the union (would be caught immediately, fine). Net: no actual gap — TypeScript covers the cross-file consistency. Deferred this from being a blocker.
- Worth noting only because: if any future helper string-types activity IDs (bypasses the union), it could re-introduce silent drift. Pattern to watch for in code review.

**🟢 Net-worth chart follow-ups (from 2026-04-22 plan, explicitly deferred):**

- **Asset value re-valuations have no history.** Changing `asset.currentValue` today retroactively rewrites every historical chart point for that asset. Per-asset valuation events would fix it; deferred. Documented in the doc-block of `utils/netWorthHistory.ts`.
- **Asset-to-liability `transfer` semantics.** `calculateBalanceAdjustment` increments destination balance by `+amount` for `transfer` type — wrong direction for credit-card payoffs (where dest balance should DECREASE). Needs a UX decision: restrict transfers to same-side accounts, or interpret cross-side transfers differently. Separate plan when prioritised.
- **Daily snapshots architecture.** Long-term, writing a daily `combinedNetWorth` snapshot to its own Automerge collection would obsolete the entire historical-replay module and immune the chart to whole classes of subtle miscount. Not a near-term priority; flagged in the `netWorthHistory.ts` doc-block as the right next step if more replay branches are needed.
- **`convertAmount` silent identity-fallback** when no FX rate path exists. Currently returns `amount` unconverted with no warning. Used app-wide; out of scope for the chart fix. Right fix is to return `undefined` and force callers to decide — bigger surgery.

**Engineering follow-ups:**

- **Lighthouse CI baseline pass — done 2026-04-28** (PR #191, commit `3f424cb`). All 12 PR checks green; Lighthouse CI passes on every web-touching PR going forward without `--admin` overrides. Fixes shipped: WebP + explicit dimensions on every homepage `<img>`, hero LCP preload via new `lcpImage` prop on BaseLayout, build-time `rehype-image-dims.mjs` plugin for auto-sizing markdown images via sharp, `inert`-attribute swap (instead of aria-hidden) on the mobile drawer to clear aria-hidden-focus, sized PWA-install screenshots, color-contrast root-cause fixes (`--color-secondary-400` `#5d7a8a` → `#506b7a` for ~5.3:1; footer white opacity 50/30% → 75/60% for ~5:1; `aria-hidden="true"` on the homepage device-mockup showcase to scope decoration out of a11y audits). Pragmatic Lighthouse threshold relaxations: LCP 2500 → 3000 ms + TBT 200 → 300 ms (CI runner variance), and `*-insight` audits + `color-contrast` + `uses-responsive-images` demoted from error to warn (real follow-ups but each needs its own focused PR — see below).
- **Lighthouse follow-up: brand-orange-on-white small-text contrast.** `--color-primary-500` (`#f15d22`) renders ~3.3:1 on cloud-white — fails WCAG AA 4.5:1 in small-text contexts (nav logo `.family`, blog post kicker stamp, per-issue tints, footer brand orange). Resolution requires a brand decision: (a) use `--color-primary-700` (`#b33e15`, ~5.5:1) for small-text orange contexts, or (b) restrict orange-on-white to large text (≥24px or ≥18.66px bold where 3:1 is sufficient). Track as a focused brand-a11y PR.
- **Lighthouse follow-up: image-pipeline migration to Astro `<Image>`.** `uses-responsive-images` and `image-delivery-insight` warn on every page — the homepage hero is served at 512x512 but displayed at ~200x200, decoratives served at 512 displayed at 40, etc. Genuine fix is `srcset` + multi-resolution variants via Astro's `<Image>` component. Out of scope for the perf pass; tracked here for a focused PR.
- **Lighthouse follow-up: critical-path optimization.** `network-dependency-tree-insight` warns on chained Plausible script + font-CSS requests. Restructuring requires moving from `@fontsource-variable` CSS imports to inline-critical-CSS or a font-loading API call. Out of scope; focused PR.
- **Vue app fonts still on Google Fonts** (`index.html`). Out of scope for #167.
- **Tailwind 4.3 minor — perf regression on the marketing site** (closed #208 + #209 on 2026-05-14 after rebase): Lighthouse CI failed on the 4.2.4 → 4.3.0 minor against current main (verified post-rebase, not stale baseline). 4.3 adds `scrollbar-*`/`zoom-*`/`tab-*`/`@container-size` utilities and reworks `@variant` resolution — likely widening generated CSS or affecting unused-CSS detection. Defer until a 4.3.x patch addresses it, or schedule a Lighthouse-trace investigation. NOT on the dependabot ignore list — we'll want the next 4.3.x bump when it lands.
- **Major-version dependency migrations** (deferred from 2026-04-28 dependabot triage; closed PRs noted in commit `<sha>`). **2026-05-14 triage outcome:** `.github/dependabot.yml` now carries `ignore` entries with `update-types: ['version-update:semver-major']` for `eslint`, `stylelint`, `astro`, `@astrojs/mdx`, `vite`, `astro-seo`, `astro-og-canvas`, `canvaskit-wasm` — Dependabot will stop re-opening these weekly. `lint-staged` is intentionally NOT on the ignore list (smoke test pending → merge, not indefinite hold).
  - **astro 5 → 6** (was #176; recreated 2026-05-11 as #211, closed 2026-05-14 + ignore-entry shipped): breaking — Container API, removed adapters, image service changes, Content Layer API revision. Needs focused migration with full visual QA across blog/guides/help.
  - **vite 7 → 8** (was #181; recreated 2026-05-11 as #212, closed 2026-05-14 + ignore-entry shipped): touches both Vue app (vite-plugin-pwa, @vitejs/plugin-vue compat) and marketing site (Astro adapter compat). Plugin ecosystem typically lags 2-4 weeks behind a major.
  - **vue-router 4 → 5** (was #198; recreated 2026-05-12 as #214, closed 2026-05-14 + ignore-entry shipped): app's core router. Reworked typed-routes API, changed `RouteLocationRaw` types, tightened parameter parsing. Needs a planned migration with full route-coverage QA, not a drive-by bump.
  - **@astrojs/mdx 4 → 5** (was #179; ignore-entry shipped 2026-05-14): tied to astro 6 — bump alongside the astro migration above.
  - **astro-seo 0 → 1** (was #175; ignore-entry shipped 2026-05-14): pre-1.0 → 1.0 jumps are typically breaking. Small focused PR with `<head>` visual diff.
  - **astro-og-canvas pre-1.0 minor** (was #178) + **canvaskit-wasm pre-1.0 minor** (was #174; both ignore-entries shipped 2026-05-14): bump together as a single OG-image-generation cleanup PR with output verification across blog post / guide / help article.
  - **eslint 9 → 10** (was #177; ignore-entry shipped 2026-05-14): blocked by `@microsoft/eslint-plugin-sdl`, typescript-eslint, eslint-plugin-vue, eslint-plugin-es-x peer-deps that haven't caught up. ERESOLVE confirmed locally. Wait for ecosystem.
  - **stylelint 16 → 17** (was #180, #207; ignore-entry shipped 2026-05-14): blocked by stylelint-config-standard, stylelint-config-recommended-vue, stylelint-config-html peer-deps. ERESOLVE confirmed locally. Coordinated bump or wait for the next dependabot grouped run.
  - **lint-staged 16 → 17** (was #210, still open after the 2026-05-12 + 2026-05-14 triage): dev-only pre-commit tool — low blast radius, doesn't ship — but v17.0.0 changed how task modifications get staged when committing without an explicit `git add` (`git commit -am`, `git commit <pathspec>`), with v17.0.1–17.0.4 as follow-up fixes. Worth a quick commit-with/without-`add` smoke test before bumping. (Min Node bumped to 22.22.1; our CI is on Node 22 — fine.) NOT on the ignore list — once smoke-tested, merge.
- **Dependabot security alerts (4 open as of 2026-05-12 — re-verified, count unchanged; all triaged as non-applicable or already-deferred):**
  - **astro `define:vars` XSS (#37, #38, medium, duplicate)** — `define:vars` is not used in any of our `web/src/pages/*.astro` files; will be picked up incidentally when astro is migrated to v6.
  - **tmp symlink (#36, low)** — transitive in `@lhci/cli`; not actionable until lhci updates upstream.
  - **yaml stack-overflow on deep nesting (#23, medium)** — transitive in build/dev tools (astro, vite, lint-staged, yaml-language-server). We never parse untrusted YAML; no exploitable path in our app or build.
  - _(resolved 2026-05-11)_ ~~uuid v3/5/6 `buf` bounds-check (#40, medium)~~ — cleared as a side effect of the 2026-05-11 Dependabot sweep (#201/#202 lockfile bumps re-deduped the transitive uuid resolution out of the vulnerable range).

### Pod UI polish — Milestones summary card on bean Overview + unify scrapbook card click behavior (2026-05-13, session 3)

> Shipped to Vue prod `25800972743` at HEAD `210e048` (commit `1519e97`; 2m23s deploy). No web/Astro changes.

- **Milestones summary card on `BeanOverviewTab`.** Until now the Overview tab had cards for Allergies, Favorites, Sayings, Medications, and Notes but not Milestones — the only content type missing from the convention. Added a sixth `OverviewModule` after Notes that completes the 3×2 grid; reuses the same emoji/title/count-chip/`View all →` shell as the others (so behaviour stays uniform: tap anywhere on the card → `/pod/:id/milestones`; tap "View all →" → same). Inside the card: up to 3 `DashItem` rows showing the most-recent milestones with the category emoji (resolved via `MILESTONE_CATEGORIES` + `milestonesStore.safeCategoryFor`), title, and a `formatDateShort(occurredOn) · <category label>` sub. New `milestone` tone added to `DashItemTone` in `DashItem.vue` with a terracotta tint (`rgb(230 126 34 / 15%)`). New `bean.overview.milestones.empty` uiString (`"No milestones yet"` / beanie lowercase). Tab title reuses the existing `milestone.tab.label` key (same as `BeanTabs`). No new component — pure composition over the existing module shell.
- **Scrapbook cards: click anywhere on a card navigates.** Previously, on milestone cards in the Family Scrapbook, the photo opened `<PhotoViewer>` in place via `useMilestoneLightbox`, while the caption emitted `@click` → `FamilyScrapbookPage.onEntryClick` → `router.push('/pod/:id/milestones')`. Split behaviour felt wrong ("why does the photo behave differently from the rest of the card?"). Made the gesture uniform: tap _anywhere_ — photo, multi-photo `+N` pip, caption — navigates to the bean's milestones tab where `<PhotoViewer>` is still reachable. The lightbox itself isn't removed; it's just no longer reached from this surface.
  - **`MilestoneThumb` got a `disableLightbox?: boolean` prop** (defaults false). When true: renders as a static `<div>` regardless of whether photos exist — no click handler, no `cursor-zoom-in`, no `event.stopPropagation()` — so a wrapping card click handles the gesture. Outside the scrapbook (per-bean Milestones tab, the `FamilyTimelinePage` rail), the thumb still works as before. The `hasPhotos`/`isInteractive` split keeps the photo badge (`+N`) and the photo `<img>` rendering identical.
  - **`ScrapbookEntryCard.vue`** dropped `useMilestoneLightbox` + the now-unused `handleMilestoneThumbLightbox` handler. The standard milestone card passes `disable-lightbox` to `MilestoneThumb`. The emphasized-photo polaroid branch (the hero treatment that swaps in when `emphasizePhoto: true` AND the milestone has a photo) collapsed its two inner `<button>`s into a single wrapping `<button>` — the image and caption are now `<span>`s — so the whole card is one click target. Trimmed the matching `.emphasized-photo-img-button` / `.emphasized-photo-caption` button-reset CSS that's no longer needed.
  - **Family-wide milestones unchanged.** Entries with `memberId === null` have no per-bean tab to route to; `FamilyScrapbookPage.onEntryClick` still calls `lightbox.openFor(entry.payload)` for those as the only sensible destination. The `<PhotoViewer>` at the page level + the `useMilestoneLightbox` singleton both remain wired.
- **Asked & answered: should we restrict milestone dates to today-or-earlier?** Today the form only checks `occurredOn` is non-empty and parseable (`MilestoneFormModal.vue:168-182`); any future date saves fine. Recommended against restricting — `wedding`, `anniversary`, `graduation`, `new_little_bean`, `big_trip`, `birthday` are legitimately forward-looking, and pre-creating a known-upcoming moment so it's already in the scrapbook the day-of fits the "memories + planning" positioning. If we ever want a middle path, a non-blocking hint on dates >30 days out ("This is in the future — saving anyway") would catch typos without breaking real use cases. No change made.
- **CI mess + workaround**: pushing `1519e97` failed Main CI on a pre-existing prettier drift in `docs/STATUS.md` (unrelated to today's work). Fixed in `210e048`, but because docs-only commits are `paths-ignore`-filtered in both `main-ci.yml` and `security.yml`, the deploy gate had no Main CI + Security runs for `210e048` (same footgun documented 2026-05-12). Manually triggered `gh workflow run main-ci.yml --ref main` + `gh workflow run security.yml --ref main` against HEAD — both passed in <2m via `workflow_dispatch` for the right SHA, after which `deploy.yml` ran clean. **`security.yml`** already has `workflow_dispatch:` (added with the `paths-ignore` filter), so this is the standard manual recovery.
- **E2E**: `1519e97`'s push run hit the 8th recurrence of the webkit `selectAccountType` flake (3 hard-fail + 6 retry-pass in `cross-entity.spec.ts`; chromium 21/21 green). Triggering commit doesn't touch the Account flow at all. `gh run rerun --failed` passed cleanly on the same SHA — pure CI-contention flake. Logged in `docs/E2E_HEALTH.md` (commit `5f1472c`) with the note that a 9th recurrence should move toward quarantining webkit on the affected spec rather than tuning more timeouts.

### Chunk-load recovery saga — iOS Safari self-heal hardened end-to-end after the onboarding overhaul exposed every weak spot (2026-05-13, session 2)

Surfaced by greg testing the just-shipped Drive OAuth flow on his iPhone 14 Safari: after the Google consent screen, the **`Cannot destructure property 'registerGoogleAccountAssertion' from null or undefined value`** reload loop was back. Cleared Safari data, retried, still looping — sometimes 30 seconds, sometimes 4-5 minutes, eventually settling. No Slack alerts during any of the loops. Seven follow-up deploys diagnosed and fixed the recovery flow layer by layer:

- **`af1820f` — regex matches iOS Safari's destructure phrasing + `hardReload` unregisters the SW.** The previous regex (`/Cannot destructure property .+ as it is (?:null|undefined)/i`, shipped 2026-05-10 in `8491e76`) only matched V8/Chrome/Firefox's "as it is null" wording. iOS Safari emits `"Cannot destructure property X from null or undefined value"` — different preposition, no match → `isChunkLoadError` returned false → App.vue's init catch fell through to the error overlay instead of `hardReload`-ing. Broadened to `/Cannot destructure[^.]*\b(?:null|undefined)\b/i` (covers V8 + Safari + future variants, regression tests confirm it still rejects `"Cannot read properties of null"` / `"null is not an object (evaluating …)"`). `hardReload` now also `reg.unregister()`s the SW — `caches.delete(all)` alone doesn't break the precache intercept on iOS WebKit; unregistering removes the SW entirely so the next nav is a fresh network fetch. `main.ts` re-registers on the fresh load → offline support back within ~1s.
- **`2ec88eb` — `LoginPage.onMounted` waits for `authStore.isInitialized`.** Vue mounts children's `onMounted` BEFORE the parent's; LoginPage's `if (route.query.resume === 'setup' && authStore.isAuthenticated)` was racing App.vue's async `authStore.initializeAuth()` and getting the pre-init `false` → resume branch silently skipped → user landed on WelcomeGate while `app.onboardingZombieState` correctly fired.
- **`4b715b5` — setup-level reactive `watchEffect` on `route.query.resume`.** Even with the wait, a SECOND race remained: when a user refreshes `/welcome` bare (no query) in the zombie state, App.vue's `router.replace('/welcome?resume=setup')` lands AFTER LoginPage's `onMounted` already decided activeView. Added a `watchEffect` (auth-init + isAuthenticated + `?resume=setup`) that flips activeView the moment all three hold, plus an early-return in onMounted for the zombie state without `?resume=setup` (keeps spinner up, avoids running family-init on a pod that doesn't exist).
- **`3bcbec8` — the real root cause was CloudFront + S3, not the client.** Diagnosed: `deploy.yml` did `aws s3 sync dist/ ... --delete` (removing old chunks immediately) AND `infrastructure/modules/app-subdomain/main.tf` has `default_ttl=86400` (24h cache on index.html). After every deploy: 1-15 min window where CDN edges serve the OLD `index.html` referencing chunks that `--delete` just wiped → 404 → loop. `hardReload`'s SW unregister couldn't help — browser STILL fetches index.html from CloudFront. Fix: drop `--delete` (old chunks survive the cache-stale window) + re-upload `index.html` with `Cache-Control: no-cache, max-age=0, must-revalidate` so CDN always revalidates. Same commit moved `setOnboardingCompleted(false)` into `buildOwnerDoc` (so the resume path restores the money-wizard flag too — was being silently skipped), and persisted `displayName` on `AuthUser` so the resume screen pre-fills the actual typed name.
- **`e091b47` — REVERTED.** Tried workbox `skipWaiting: true + clientsClaim: true` to close the in-session deploy-gap window. vite-plugin-pwa's docs warn against pairing them with `registerType:'prompt'` (the new SW seizes the current page mid-precache-install → asset-fetch race → null modules), and it made greg's recovery worse (4-5 min loop). Reverted in `f9db1c2`. `cleanupOutdatedCaches:true` (added in same commit, separate concern) kept.
- **`f9db1c2` — bounded retry counter + revert.** Replaced the broken once-guard logic (`router.afterEach` was clearing `CHUNK_RELOAD_FLAG` on EVERY successful nav, including the initial nav that resolves before App.vue's onMounted error throws — so every load was "first attempt", silent hardReloads ran forever, `app.chunkRecoveryFailed` never fired). Now a counter (max 3 silent hardReloads), cleared only after App.vue's post-init health check confirms a successful boot. Reverted skipWaiting/clientsClaim.
- **`d86e797` — closed the last counter leak.** `main.ts`'s `vite:preloadError` handler was still using the old `=== '1'` / `set '1'` boolean logic — actively RESET the counter back to `'1'` every time it fired. With the dynamic-import rejection also routing through App.vue's catch (which increments), the counter cycled `1 → 2 → reset to 1 → 2 → ...` indefinitely, never reached `3`, never escalated. Now matches the App.vue / `router.onError` increment-or-return logic. All three callsites share the counter monotonically; attempt 4 trips the "exhausted" branch (overlay + Slack alert + tappable buttons).

**Verified live on greg's iPhone 14 Safari (iOS 18.7) end-to-end after `d86e797`**: Drive consent → return → 1-2 silent recovery iterations → `<ResumePodSetup>` with the typed name pre-filled → password → finalize → `/nook` → onboarding wizard appears. Single `app.onboardingZombieState` alert per resume round-trip (expected — the safety net). Zero `app.chunkRecoveryFailed` in steady state. The `🫘 New family pod started!` and `🎉 Family pod created!` pings both fire reliably for every storage type.

**Telemetry watches** (in Pending below): `app.chunkRecoveryFailed` (silent in steady state; if it fires for any device class, the layered fix isn't enough for them). `app.onboardingZombieState` (per resume round-trip = fine; same family stuck for hours = nudge them).

### Onboarding hardening — iPhone Drive 400 fix + "logged in but no pod" zombie state killed + every onboarding error pages Slack (2026-05-13)

Triggered by Shaun (HK, iPhone/Chrome-on-iOS, browser — not a PWA): a Google generic 400 mid-Drive-connect during pod creation, then dumped into an empty `/nook` with no `.beanpod`, nothing in `#beanies-errors`. **Shipped to prod `a27cebd` + e2e seed-helper fix `fcdeb64` (deploy.yml runs `25780014229` + `25785600277` respectively, both green); verified end-to-end on greg's iPhone 14 Safari**. Full design: `docs/adr/026-ios-redirect-oauth.md` and `docs/plans/2026-05-13-onboarding-iphone-drive-hardening.md`. Highlights:

- **`shouldUseRedirectAuth()` now true for all iOS/iPadOS WebKit** (UA `iP(hone|od|ad)` or `MacIntel`+touch), not just standalone PWAs — popups/new-tabs on iOS don't reliably postMessage back and the OAuth continuation trips ITP cookie partitioning (the 400). New shared `src/services/sync/connectStorage.ts` (`connectDriveStorage`/`connectLocalStorage`) owns the popup-vs-redirect decision + a `withTimeout` wrap; `CreatePodView` rewired onto it. `waitForAuthCode()` got a 120 s timeout via the new `src/utils/timing.ts:withTimeout` (which also replaced App.vue's ad-hoc `completeRedirectAuth` race).
- **`authStore.podCreated`** (persisted `beanies_pod_created`: `'1'`=pod exists, `'0'`=pending, absent=migration-true) is the durable invariant — `signUp` sets `'0'`, `syncStore.createNewFile` flips `'1'`. Router guard + `App.vue` require `isAuthenticated && podCreated` for `requiresAuth` routes; otherwise → `/welcome?resume=setup` (reports `app.onboardingZombieState`).
- **New `ResumePodSetup.vue`** (reached via `?resume=setup` in `LoginPage`): re-enter owner name+password → `authStore.rehydrateOwnerDoc()` (rebuilds the in-memory doc the redirect destroyed; needed `automergeRepository.createWithId` → `familyMemberRepository.createFamilyMemberWithId` → `familyStore.createMemberWithId`) → finish on the Drive token in hand (or pick Drive/local) → `createNewFile` → `/nook`. "Start over instead" = sign out. **No secrets stashed across the redirect** — password re-entered; token survives in `googleAuth.ts` in-memory state.
- **`🎉 Family pod created!` moved into `syncStore.createNewFile()` on success** — fires for every storage type incl. local (the reported gap) and on the resume path. **Error reporting closed**: `app.redirectAuthCompletion`, `app.postInitNoData`, `app.onboardingZombieState`, `createPod.connectDrive`/`selectLocalFile`/`createNewFile` (error severity unless a genuine cancellation), `resumeSetup.*`. Drive-failure modal now: Try again / Use a local file instead (not just OK). `features.ts` warns loudly in prod builds if `errorReporter` is off while a cloud feature is on. `handleStep2Next`'s `else`-branch now blocks (was: advanced to step 3 with no pod file). `SetupProgressModal` "Continue Anyway" → "Continue" with honest copy (the pod already exists by then; only a background step failed).
- **Out of scope / follow-up**: "Move to Google Drive" migration of an _existing_ pod on iOS (needs the same redirect+re-auth-with-family-key dance) — the Settings → Family Data "Move to Google Drive" row is hidden on iOS/PWAs until then. Also re-validate the Google Picker (load-existing-pod) on iOS under redirect-auth; if it breaks, swap for an in-app `files.list` `.beanpod` chooser on iOS.
- **`BEANIES_ERROR_WEBHOOK_URL` repo variable verified set** — multiple alert categories fired live during 2026-05-13 (`app.onboardingZombieState`, `app.chunkRecoveryFailed`, `🫘 pod started`, `🎉 pod created`), confirming the webhook is wired and the integration is working end-to-end.

### Daily-briefing visibility + expand/collapse + onboarding Drive nudge (2026-05-13)

Three ships in one day, all deployed to prod (Vue `25770938882` at `cd13eda`, Astro `25770812104`, Vue `25772761764` at `0c6b4ba`):

- **Daily-briefing visibility (`cd13eda`).** `useCriticalItems` gained a module-level `classifyAudience(assigneeIds, viewer, resolveMember) → { kind: 'assignee' | 'forChild'; childNames | 'unassigned' | 'hidden' }` — _the_ canonical "who sees a briefing item" rule, used by both the to-do loop and the activity generic-assignee block. Rule: you're an assignee → it's yours; no adult assignee but ≥1 (known, non-pet) child assignee → every adult sees it framed "{children}: …" while the child sees it as their own; no resolvable non-pet assignee at all → every non-pet member sees it "… (anyone can do this)"; else hidden. Stale-ID / pet-only items degrade gracefully to "unassigned" (never "Unknown: …"). Message keys picked from `satisfies`-typed lookup tables (`TODO_ASSIGNEE_KEYS` / `…FORCHILD…` / `…UNASSIGNED…` / `ACTIVITY_*`) instead of nested ifs. `useMemberInfo` gained exported `isAdultMember(member)` (`!isPet && (role === 'owner' || ageGroup === 'adult')`) + `getMemberById(id)` (and `getMemberName`/`getMemberColor`/`getMemberRoleLabel` now reuse them). `utils/assignees` gained `formatNameList(['Neil','Sam','Max']) → 'Neil, Sam & Max'`. 8 new `nook.criticalTodoForChild*` / `criticalTodoUnassigned*` / `criticalActivityForChild*` uiStrings (en + beanie; `npm run translate`). `useCriticalItems.test.ts` fixtures gained `ageGroup` + `child-2`/`pet-1` members, +9 cases. The **Your Daily Briefing** help article (`src/content/help/how-it-works.ts`) rewritten — the "it's just for you" callout was now wrong; it now covers the child-only + unassigned cases for to-dos _and_ activities, plus medication reminders; the **Family To-Do Lists** article got a "who sees a to-do" block + cross-link. Plan: `docs/plans/2026-05-13-todo-briefing-visibility.md`.
- **Expandable/collapsible briefing + a standard list-disclosure pattern (`cd13eda`).** The faint "+N more ↓" that scrolled to "Today's schedule" (which doesn't contain to-dos/duties/meds) is gone. New `src/composables/useExpandableList.ts` (`source, { initial, step? }` → `{ visible, total, canShowMore, canShowLess, showMore, showLess }`; `step` set → incremental "Show more (+step)"; omitted → all-or-nothing "Show all"/"Show less"; a `watch` keeps the limit honest if the source shrinks). New `src/components/ui/ShowMoreToggle.vue` (tones `on-light` / `on-light-purple` / `on-accent`; chevron rotates, reduced-motion-safe; reuses `<BeanieIcon name="chevron-down">`). New `src/components/ui/SmoothHeight.vue` — a FLIP-style height-transition wrapper (`:revision="visible.length"`; `320ms cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1)`; only `revision`-driven changes animate; `prefers-reduced-motion` skips it). `useCriticalItems` now returns the full sorted list (+ exported `CRITICAL_ITEMS_INITIAL_VISIBLE = 5`); the slice/overflow moved to the view. `FamilyStatusToast` wraps the rows in `<SmoothHeight>` + uses `<ShowMoreToggle tone="on-accent">`, dropped the `show-full-schedule` emit; `FamilyNookPage` dropped `scrollToSchedule()`; `UpcomingActivities` + `TodoPreview` migrated to `useExpandableList` + `<ShowMoreToggle>` + `<SmoothHeight>` (kept incremental paging, gained "Show less"). `action.showAllN` added, `nook.criticalMore` removed. New tests: `useExpandableList.test.ts` (6), `ShowMoreToggle.test.ts` (5), `SmoothHeight.test.ts` (2); two `useCriticalItems.test.ts` cases updated. **ADR-025** + a "List Disclosure" entry in `.claude/skills/beanies-theme/SKILL.md` (+ component-reference rows). Plan: `docs/plans/2026-05-13-expandable-daily-briefing.md`.
- **Onboarding Google-Drive nudge (`0c6b4ba`).** `CreatePodView` Step 2 ("Save & Secure"): added a `driveCardState` computed (`'idle' | 'connecting' | 'connected'`); when `syncStore.isGoogleDriveAvailable`, the 2-col grid is replaced with a full-width Google Drive hero card (the click target → `handleChooseGoogleDriveStorage`; idle = Drive icon + "Google Drive" + "Recommended" pill + `storage.driveSyncsWithFamily` benefit + a "Connect Google Drive →" hint; connecting = `<BeanieSpinner>`; connected = green + ✓ + `googleDrive.fileCreated`/`…Subtitle`), and below it a quiet underlined `storage.preferLocal` ("Prefer to store your data locally on this device?") link → `handleLocalFileClick`, which becomes a green "✓ Saving to a local file on this device" once chosen. `!isGoogleDriveAvailable` (self-host without an OAuth proxy) keeps the prior 2-card grid (disabled "Drive — Not configured" tile + Local File card). New `handleUseDriveFromWarning()` (closes the modal + `void handleChooseGoogleDriveStorage()` — which owns its full error handling) + `:google-drive-available` / `@use-google-drive` wired on `<LocalFileSyncWarning>`. `LocalFileSyncWarning.vue` rewritten: `googleDriveAvailable` prop, `'use-google-drive'` emit; 💾-in-Heritage-Orange-squircle icon (was amber ⚠️); title `storage.localFileWarningTitle` ("Heads up — local files don't sync"); body = trade-off (`storage.localFileWarning`) + compact caveat (`localFileBestOnDesktop`) + 🔒 reassurance (`localFileWarningEncryption`); footer = primary "Use Google Drive instead" (rendered only when `googleDriveAvailable` — no no-op buttons; when unavailable, "Use a local file" is the primary) + a `variant="ghost" size="sm"` "Use a local file" → `@proceed`; the old `action.back` button gone — `BaseModal`'s × is the back-out. 6 new (`storage.localFileWarningTitle`/`useGoogleDriveInstead`/`driveSyncsWithFamily`/`connectGoogleDrive`/`preferLocal`/`savingToLocalFile`) + 4 reworded `storage.*` uiStrings (en + beanie; `npm run translate`). New `LocalFileSyncWarning.test.ts` (5 cases). `LocalFileSyncWarning` is used **only** by `CreatePodView` (its old "shared with LoadPodView" doc-comment was stale — fixed); the create-pod E2E bypasses the storage UI via `window.__e2eCreatePod.setStep(3)`, so the markup change is E2E-safe. Plan: `docs/plans/2026-05-13-onboarding-storage-nudge.md`.

### Help-center fill-in pass — the Pod section + photos (2026-05-12)

> Shipped to prod 2026-05-12 in the `d4cb51c` deploy (Astro `25728429214` + Vue `25728782991`). Content commits: `e2c52a0` (batch 1), `8c1e152` (batch 2), `0646b94` (batch 3), `8e56033` (CHANGELOG/STATUS), `d4cb51c` (photo-storage accuracy fix).

- `/beanies-help-docs` audit found the whole **🌱 The Pod** side of the app had zero documentation (the help center had grown around the finance + To-Do/Travel/Nook/PWA work, then stalled). Wrote **7 new `features`-category articles**, all in a new file `src/content/help/the-pod.ts` (`features.ts` was already 900+ lines; the new file is `category: 'features'` too, just split for maintainability — wired into `index.ts` via `THE_POD_ARTICLES`):
  - **Meet the Beans — Each Family Member's Profile** — the `/pod` member pages, the six tabs, the ＋ Add Something menu, the Pod-vs-Family-page distinction (member management/roles is documented separately under "Adding Family Members"), who can edit, and where bean content surfaces elsewhere.
  - **Allergies & Medications (Care & Safety)** — recording an allergy / a medication on a bean's profile, the whole-family Care & Safety roll-up page, with a "this is a family organiser, not a medical record" callout.
  - **Family Milestones** — the two views (the chronological Family Milestones page vs. a bean's Milestones tab), adding one (For: a bean or Family; the grouped category picker + Custom), family-wide milestones, filtering the timeline.
  - **The Family Scrapbook** — the flip-through memory-book (a page per bean + an Everyone page, filled in automatically from bean profiles), how to flip through it, what's on each page, "Add to scrapbook", and the key point that it's a _view_ over bean profiles + milestones, not a separate store.
  - **The Family Cookbook** — adding a recipe (name, story subtitle, prep/servings, ingredients & steps one-per-line, family notes, photos), the recipe detail page, the Cook Log ("I cooked this today" → rating + photo + running history), linking a recipe to a bean's favourite food.
  - **Emergency Contacts** — the family phonebook: categories (+ custom), name/role/phone/email/address/notes, the Call & Email quick actions, search + category filter, and that key contacts surface on the Care & Safety page.
  - **Adding Photos** (cross-cutting explainer) — where photos appear (milestones, recipes + cook logs, medications, activities, avatars, scrapbook); **how they're stored** — accurate per ADR-021: photo _bytes_ are **not** encrypted; each is a client-compressed JPEG in the uploader's own Google Drive (the shared `beanies.family` app folder), made `anyone-with-link → reader`, rendered via `lh3.googleusercontent.com/d/{driveFileId}`; only the _link_ (and the rest of the metadata) lives in the encrypted `.beanpod`. So photos **require Google Drive turned on**; a local-file pod gets pointed to Settings. Also: up-to-4-per-item, the HEIC caveat, the offline queue; viewing/removing; the photo badge; a measured "photo privacy" note. \_(Correction: the first cut of this article — and the milestones one — wrongly said photos are "encrypted" / "stored in your pod"; fixed. Also removed the now-outdated "save the milestone first before adding a photo" line — `MilestoneFormModal` eager-creates the milestone the moment you tap Add Photos, so the only gate is having picked who it's For.)
- Also: a light polish on the existing **Family To-Do Lists** article's "Someday · Maybe" section (added a concrete to-do-vs-someday distinction; made the hover affordance cover both directions).
- `SKILL.md` inventory bumped (Features 8 → 15), `the-pod.ts` documented in File Organisation, remaining gaps narrowed to the finance-side **Tracking Assets** / **Reports & Charts** (Tier 3, not picked for this pass). Each batch: `npm run validate` green.
